task sum_Of_Primes(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r == sum_of_primes(n)
spec fun is_prime(k: int): bool
  decreases k
= if k < 2 then false else if k == 2 then true else if k % 2 == 0 then false else is_prime_odd(k, 3)
spec fun is_prime_odd(k: int, d: int): bool
  decreases k - d
= if d * d > k then true else if k % d == 0 then false else is_prime_odd(k, d + 2)
spec fun sum_of_primes(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n < 2 then 0 else if is_prime(n) then n + sum_of_primes(n - 1) else sum_of_primes(n - 1)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 2;
  while i <= n
    invariant r == sum_of_primes(i - 1)
    invariant i >= 2 and i <= n + 1
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    if is_prime(i) {
      r := r + i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

t 1
gate recursion
task recur_gcd(a: int, b: int) returns (r: int)
  requires a >= 0
  requires b >= 0
  ensures r == gcds(a, b)
  decreases a + b
spec fun gcds(a: int, b: int): int
  decreases a + b
= if a <= 0 then b else if b <= 0 then a else if a > b then gcds(a - b, b) else if b > a then gcds(a, b - a) else a
{
  if a == 0 {
    r := b;
  } else {
    if b == 0 {
      r := a;
    } else {
      if a > b {
        r := recur_gcd(a - b, b);
      } else {
        if b > a {
          r := recur_gcd(a, b - a);
        } else {
          r := a;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

= if i >= n - 1 then 0 else if i + s[i] < n - 1 then ...

task is_sublist(s: seq, sub: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (exists i in [0, len(s) - len(sub) + 1) . (forall j in [0, len(sub)) . j < len(sub) and i + j < len(s) and s[i + j] == sub[j]))
{
  r := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i <= len(s) - len(sub) and not r
    invariant (forall k in [0, i) . k <= len(s) - len(sub) ==> (forall j in [0, len(sub)) . j < len(sub) and k + j < len(s) and s[k + j] == sub[j])) ==> false
    invariant r ==> (exists k in [0, i) . k <= len(s) - len(sub) and (forall j in [0, len(sub)) . j < len(sub) and k + j < len(s) and s[k + j] == sub[j]))
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s) - len(sub) + 1
    decreases len(s) - len(sub) + 1 - i
  {
    var j: int := 0;
    var match: bool := true;
    while j < len(sub) and match
      invariant match ==> (forall k in [0, j) . k < len(sub) and i + k < len(s) and s[i + k] == sub[k])
      invariant not match ==> (exists k in [0, j) . k < len(sub) and (i + k >= len(s) or s[i + k] != sub[k]))
      invariant j >= 0 and j <= len(sub)
      decreases len(sub) - j
    {
      if i + j >= len(s) or s[i + j] != sub[j] {
        match := false;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if match {
      r := true;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == (fact_mod(n, 100))
  ensures 0 <= r and r < 100
spec fun fact_mod(n: int, m: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then 1 else (fact_mod(n - 1, m) * n) % m
{
  r := 1;
  var i: int := 2;
  while i <= n
    invariant r == (fact_mod(i - 1, 100))
    invariant 0 <= r and r < 100
    invariant i >= 2 and i <= n + 1
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    r := (r * i) % 100;
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task equilibrium_index(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == -1 or (r >= 0 and r < len(s) and (forall i in [0, r) . s[i]) + 0 == (forall i in [r + 1, len(s)) . s[i]) + 0)
  ensures r == -1 ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . (forall j in [0, i) . s[j]) + 0 != (forall j in [i + 1, len(s)) . s[j]) + 0)
  ensures r != -1 ==> (forall i in [0, r) . (forall j in [0, i) . s[j]) + 0 != (forall j in [i + 1, len(s)) . s[j]) + 0)
{
  r := -1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and r == -1
    invariant r == -1 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . (forall k in [0, j) . s[k]) + 0 != (forall k in [j + 1, len(s)) . s[k]) + 0)
    invariant r != -1 ==> r >= 0 and r < len(s) and (forall k in [0, r) . s[k]) + 0 == (forall k in [r + 1, len(s)) . s[k]) + 0 and (forall j in [0, r) . (forall k in [0, j) . s[k]) + 0 != (forall k in [j + 1, len(s)) . s[k]) + 0)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    var left: int := 0;
    var li: int := 0;
    while li < i
      invariant left == (forall k in [0, li) . s[k]) + 0
      invariant li >= 0 and li <= i
      decreases i - li
    {
      left := left + s[li];
      li := li + 1;
    }
    var right: int := 0;
    var ri: int := i + 1;
    while ri < len(s)
      invariant right == (forall k in [ri, len(s)) . s[k]) + 0
      invariant ri >= i + 1 and ri <= len(s)
      decreases len(s) - ri
    {
      right := right + s[ri];
      ri := ri + 1;
    }
    if left == right {
      r := i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task is_subset(a: seq, n: int, b: seq, m: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires n >= 0
  requires m >= 0
  requires n <= len(a)
  requires m <= len(b)
  ensures r == (forall i in [0, m) . exists j in [0, n) . b[i] == a[j])
{
  r := true;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < m and r
    invariant r ==> (forall k in [0, i) . exists j in [0, n) . b[k] == a[j])
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= m
    decreases m - i
  {
    var found: bool := false;
    var j: int := 0;
    while j < n and not found
      invariant not found ==> (forall k in [0, j) . k < n ==> b[i] != a[k])
      invariant j >= 0 and j <= n
      decreases n - j
    {
      if b[i] == a[j] {
        found := true;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if found {
    } else {
      r := false;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task count_Fac(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 2
  ensures r == count_Fac_spec(n) - 1
spec fun count_Fac_spec(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then 0 else if n == 2 then 1 else if n % 2 == 0 then 1 + count_Fac_spec(n / 2) else count_Fac_spec(n)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 2;
  var m: int := n;
  while m > 1
    invariant m >= 1
    invariant r >= 0
    decreases m
  {
    if m % i == 0 {
      r := r + 1;
      m := m / i;
    } else {
      i := i + 1;
    }
  }
  r := r - 1;
}

task left_insertion(s: seq, x: int) returns (r: int)

  ensures r <= len(s)

  ensures r == len(s) or s[r] >= x

    invariant r == i
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    invariant (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] < x)

  {
    i := i + 1;
    r := i;
  }

task find_Index(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r >= 1
  ensures r * (r + 1) >= 2 * pow10(n - 1)
  ensures (r - 1) * r < 2 * pow10(n - 1)
spec fun pow10(e: int): int
  decreases e
= if e <= 0 then 1 else 10 * pow10(e - 1)
{
  var p: int := 1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n - 1
    invariant p == pow10(i)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= n - 1
    decreases n - 1 - i
  {
    i := i + 1;
    p := p * 10;
  }
  var k: int := 1;
  while k * (k + 1) < 2 * p
    invariant k >= 1
    invariant (k - 1) * k < 2 * p
    decreases 0
  {
    k := k + 1;
  }
  r := k;
}

task check_min_heap(s: seq, start: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires start >= 0
  requires start < len(s)

{
  r := true;
  var i: int := start;
  while i < len(s) and r
    invariant (forall j in [start, i) . (2 * j + 1 < len(s) ==> s[j] ==> s[j] <= s[2 * j + 1]) and (2 * j + 2 < len(s) ==> s[j] <= s[2 * j + 2]))
    invariant i >= start and i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if 2 * i + 1 < len(s) and s[i] > s[2 * i + 1] {
      r := false;
    } else {
    }
    if 2 * i + 2 < len(s) and s[i] > s[2 * i + 2] {
      r := false;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

{
  if n == 0 {
    r := 0;

    if n == 1 {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      var a: int := jacobsthal_num(n - 1);

      r := a + 2 * b;
    }
  }
}

  requires n >= 1

  var k: int := 1;
  var lim: int := if m < n then m else n;
  while k <= lim
    invariant r == (var s: int := 0; var j: int := 1; while j < k { s := s + (m - j + 1) * (n - j + 1); j := j + 1; } s)
    invariant k >= 1 and k <= lim + 1
    decreases lim - k + 1
  {
    r := r + (m - k + 1) * (n - k + 1);
    k := k + 1;
  }
}

task get_Pairs_Count(arr: seq, n: int, target: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires n <= len(arr)
  ensures r == (forall i in [0, n) . (forall j in [i+1, n) . true) ==> (
    (forall i in [0, n) . (forall j in [i+1, n) . (arr[i] + arr[j] == target ==> true)))
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r == (
    (forall i in [0, n) . (forall j in [i+1, n) . (arr[i] + arr[j] == target ==> true)))
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n - 1
    invariant r == (
      (forall a in [0, i) . (forall b in [a+1, n) . (arr[a] + arr[b] == target ==> true)))
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= n - 1
    decreases n - 1 - i
  {
    var j: int := i + 1;
    while j < n
      invariant r == (
        (forall a in [0, i) . (forall b in [a+1, n) . (arr[a] + arr[b] == target ==> true)))
        + (forall b in [i+1, j) . (arr[i] + arr[b] == target ==> true))
      invariant j >= i + 1 and j <= n
      decreases n - j
    {
      if arr[i] + arr[j] == target {
        r := r + 1;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

gate loops
task is_Diff(n: int) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (diff_even_odd(n) % 2 == 0)
spec fun diff_even_odd(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 0 then 0 else (if n % 10 % 2 == 0 then n % 10 else -n % 10) + diff_even_odd(n / 10)
{
  var even_sum: int := 0;
  var odd_sum: int := 0;
  var m: int := n;
  if m < 0 {
    m := -m;
  }
  if m == 0 {
    r := true;
  } else {
    while m > 0
      invariant even_sum >= 0
      invariant odd_sum >= 0
      invariant m >= 0
      decreases m
    {
      var d: int := m % 10;
      if d % 2 == 0 {
        even_sum := even_sum + d;
      } else {
        odd_sum := odd_sum + d;
      }
      m := m / 10;
    }
    var diff: int := even_sum - odd_sum;
    if diff % 2 == 0 {
      r := true;
    } else {
      r := false;
    }
  }
}

    invariant r == (if i <= 1 then 0 else 0)

  {

    r := r + odd * odd * odd * odd;
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

  }

task Odd_Length_Sum(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == odd_len_sum(s)
spec fun odd_len_sum(s: seq): int
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) == 0 then 0 else (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i]) + odd_len_sum_rest(s, 1)
spec fun odd_len_sum_rest(s: seq, L: int): int
  decreases len(s) - L
= if L > len(s) then 0 else (forall i in [0, len(s) - L + 1) . sum_range(s, i, L)) + odd_len_sum_rest(s, L + 2)
spec fun sum_range(s: seq, i: int, L: int): int
  decreases L
= if L == 0 then 0 else s[i] + sum_range(s, i + 1, L - 1)
{
  r := 0;
  var L: int := 1;
  while L <= len(s)
    invariant r == (sum of all odd-length subarrays with length < L)
    invariant L >= 1
    decreases len(s) - L + 1
  {
    var i: int := 0;
    while i <= len(s) - L
      invariant r == (sum of all odd-length subarrays with length < L) + (sum of subarrays of length L starting at positions < i)
      invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s) - L
      decreases len(s) - L - i + 1
    {
      var j: int := 0;
      var sub_sum: int := 0;
      while j < L
        invariant sub_sum == (sum of s[i..i+j-1])
        invariant j >= 0 and j <= L
        decreases L - j
      {
        sub_sum := sub_sum + s[i + j];
        j := j + 1;
      }
      r := r + sub_sum;
      i := i + 1;
    }
    L := L + 2;
  }
}

task mul_even_odd(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  requires len(s) > 0
  ensures exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] % 2 == 0
  ensures exists j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] % 2 == 1
  ensures r == (if exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] % 2 == 0 then (if exists j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] % 2 == 1 then (let i0 = (if exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] % 2 == 0 then (if s[0] % 2 == 0 then 0 else (if exists k in [0, len(s)) . s[k] % 2 == 0 and (forall m in [0, k) . s[m] % 2 != 0) then k else 0)) else 0) in (let j0 = (if exists j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] % 2 == 1 then (if s[0] % 2 == 1 then 0 else (if exists k in [0, len(s)) . s[k] % 2 == 1 and (forall m in [0, k) . s[m] % 2 != 1) then k else 0)) else 0) in s[i0] * s[j0]) else 0) else 0)
{
  var first_even: int := 0;
  var found_even: bool := false;
  var first_odd: int := 0;
  var found_odd: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and (not found_even or not found_odd)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    invariant found_even ==> first_even >= 0 and first_even < len(s) and s[first_even] % 2 == 0 and (forall m in [0, first_even) . m < len(s) ==> s[m] % 2 != 0)
    invariant found_odd ==> first_odd >= 0 and first_odd < len(s) and s[first_odd] % 2 == 1 and (forall m in [0, first_odd) . m < len(s) ==> s[m] % 2 != 1)
    invariant not found_even ==> (forall m in [0, i) . m < len(s) ==> s[m] % 2 != 0)
    invariant not found_odd ==> (forall m in [0, i) . m < len(s) ==> s[m] % 2 != 1)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if not found_even and s[i] % 2 == 0 {
      first_even := i;
      found_even := true;
    } else {
    }
    if not found_odd and s[i] % 2 == 1 {
      first_odd := i;
      found_odd := true;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := s[first_even] * s[first_odd];
}

task right_insertion(s: seq, x: int) returns (r: int)
  requires (forall i in [0, len(s)) . (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] <= s[i]))
  ensures r >= 0 and r <= len(s)
  ensures (forall i in [0, r) . i < len(s) ==> s[i] <= x)
  ensures (forall i in [r, len(s)) . i >= 0 ==> s[i] > x)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant r >= 0 and r <= len(s)
    invariant (forall j in [0, r) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] <= x)
    invariant (forall j in [r, i) . j >= 0 and j < len(s) ==> s[j] > x)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] <= x {
      r := i + 1;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

  requires l >= 0
  requires r >= l
  ensures res == (forall i in [l, r + 1) . (i % 2 == 1 ==> 1) + (i % 2 == 0 ==> 0)) * 0 + (if l % 2 == 0 then (if r % 2 == 0 then (r - 1) * (r + 1) / 2 - (l - 1) * l / 2 else r * (r + 1) / 2 - (l - 1) * l / 2) else (if r % 2 == 0 then (r - 1) * r / 2 - (l - 1) * l / 2 else r * (r + 1) / 2 - l * (l + 1) / 2))

    invariant i >= l and i <= r + 1
    decreases r - i + 1
  {
    if i % 2 == 1 {
      res := res + i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task is_Perfect_Square(n: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == (exists k in [0, n + 1) . k * k == n)
{
  r := false;
  var k: int := 0;
  while k * k <= n and not r
    invariant (forall j in [0, k) . j * j != n) ==> not r
    invariant r ==> (exists j in [0, k) . j * j == n)
    invariant k >= 0
    decreases n + 1 - k
  {
    if k * k == n {
      r := true;
    } else {
    }
    k := k + 1;
  }
}

        r := r + 1;

    }

task max_sub_array_sum(s: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n == len(s)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r <= n
  ensures (forall i in [0, n) . (forall j in [i, n) . (j - i) <= r))
  ensures (exists i in [0, n) . (exists j in [i, n) . (j - i) == r and (forall k in [0, n) . (forall l in [k, n) . (l - k) == r ==> (sum_range(s, i, j) >= sum_range(s, k, l)))))
  ensures (forall i in [0, n) . (forall j in [i, n) . (j - i) == r ==> (forall k in [0, n) . (forall l in [k, n) . (l - k) == r ==> sum_range(s, i, j) == sum_range(s, k, l))))
spec fun sum_range(s: seq, i: int, j: int): int
  decreases j - i
= if i == j then 0 else s[i] + sum_range(s, i + 1, j)
{
  r := 0;
  var best_sum: int := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= n
    invariant r >= 0 and r <= n
    invariant (forall a in [0, i) . (forall b in [a, n) . (b - a) <= r))
    invariant (exists a in [0, i) . (exists b in [a, n) . (b - a) == r and (forall c in [0, i) . (forall d in [c, n) . (d - c) == r ==> sum_range(s, a, b) >= sum_range(s, c, d)))))
    invariant (forall a in [0, i) . (forall b in [a, n) . (b - a) == r ==> (forall c in [0, i) . (forall d in [c, n) . (d - c) == r ==> sum_range(s, a, b) == sum_range(s, c, d))))
    decreases n - i
  {
    var cur_sum: int := 0;
    var j: int := i;
    while j < n
      invariant j >= i and j <= n
      invariant cur_sum == sum_range(s, i, j)
      invariant r >= 0 and r <= n
      invariant (forall a in [0, i) . (forall b in [a, n) . (b - a) <= r))
      invariant (exists a in [0, i) . (exists b in [a, n) . (b - a) == r and (forall c in [0, i) . (forall d in [c, n) . (d - c) == r ==> sum_range(s, a, b) >= sum_range(s, c, d)))))
      invariant (forall a in [0, i) . (forall b in [a, n) . (b - a) == r ==> (forall c in [0, i) . (forall d in [c, n) . (d - c) == r ==> sum_range(s, a, b) == sum_range(s, c, d))))
      decreases n - j
    {
      cur_sum := cur_sum + s[j];
      if j - i > r {
        r := j - i;
        best_sum := cur_sum;
      } else {
        if j - i == r and cur_sum > best_sum {
          best_sum := cur_sum;
        } else {
        }
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task sequence(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r == newman(n)
  decreases n - 1
spec fun newman(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 1 then 1 else if n == 2 then 1 else newman(newman(n - 1) + 1)
{
  if n == 1 {
    r := 1;
  } else {
    if n == 2 {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      var a: int := 1;
      var b: int := 1;
      var i: int := 3;
      while i <= n
        invariant a == newman(i - 2)
        invariant b == newman(i - 1)
        invariant i >= 3 and i <= n + 1
        decreases n - i + 1
      {
        var c: int := b;
        a := b;
        b := c;
        i := i + 1;
      }
      r := b;
    }
  }
}

  requires n >= 0
  requires n <= len(s)
  ensures r == (forall i in [0, n) . (forall j in [i + 1, n) . s[i] <= s[j])) ==> 0
  ensures r == (exists i in [0, n) . (exists j in [i + 1, n) . s[i] > s[j])) ==> (exists i in [0, n) . (exists j in [i + 1, n) . s[i] > s[j]))
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r == (forall i in [0, n) . (forall j in [i + 1, n) . s[i] <= s[j])) ==> 0
  ensures (exists i in [0, n) . (exists j in [i + 1, n) . s[i] > s[j])) ==> r >= 1
  ensures r == (forall i in [0, n) . (forall j in [i + 1, n) . s[i] <= s[j])) ==> 0

task get_odd_occurence(s: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n == len(s)
  ensures exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == r and (forall j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] == r) ==> (exists k in [0, len(s)) . s[k] == r and (forall m in [0, len(s)) . s[m] == r ==> (m == k or (exists p in [0, len(s)) . s[p] == r and p != k)))
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == r ==> (exists j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] == r and j != i)
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] != r ==> (forall j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] == r ==> (exists k in [0, len(s)) . s[k] == r and k != j))
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    r := r + s[i];
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task nth_super_ugly_number(n: int, primes: seq) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  requires len(primes) >= 1
  ensures r == nth_super_ugly(n, primes)
spec fun nth_super_ugly(n: int, primes: seq): int
  decreases n
= if n == 1 then 1 else next_super_ugly(n - 1, primes)
spec fun next_super_ugly(count: int, primes: seq): int
  decreases count
= if count == 0 then 1 else min_candidate(count, primes)
spec fun min_candidate(count: int, primes: seq): int
  decreases count
= if count == 0 then 1 else min_of_products(count, primes)
spec fun min_of_products(count: int, primes: seq): int
  decreases count
= if count == 0 then 1 else min_product_at(count, primes)
spec fun min_product_at(count: int, primes: seq): int
  decreases count
= if count == 0 then 1 else min_over_primes(count, primes, 0)
spec fun min_over_primes(count: int, primes: seq, j: int): int
  decreases len(primes) - j
= if j == len(primes) then min_over_indices(count, primes, 0) else min(min_over_primes(count, primes, j + 1), min_product_for_prime(count, primes, j))
spec fun min_product_for_prime(count: int, primes: seq, j: int): int
  decreases count
= if count == 0 then 1 else min_product_for_prime_helper(count, primes, j, 0)
spec fun min_product_for_prime_helper(count: int, primes: seq, j: int, i: int): int
  decreases count - i
= if i == count then 1 else min(min_product_for_prime_helper(count, primes, j, i + 1), super_ugly_at(i, primes) * primes[j])
spec fun super_ugly_at(i: int, primes: seq): int
  decreases i
= if i == 0 then 1 else super_ugly_at_helper(i, primes, 0)
spec fun super_ugly_at_helper(i: int, primes: seq, current: int): int
  decreases i
= if current == i then current else super_ugly_at_helper(i, primes, next_ugly(current, primes))
spec fun next_ugly(current: int, primes: seq): int
  decreases 1
= min_over_all_primes(current, primes, 0)
spec fun min_over_all_primes(current: int, primes: seq, j: int): int
  decreases len(primes) - j
= if j == len(primes) then current + 1 else min(min_over_all_primes(current, primes, j + 1), current * primes[j])
{
  r := 1;
  var i: int := 1;
  while i < n
    invariant i >= 1 and i <= n
    invariant r == nth_super_ugly(i, primes)
    decreases n - i
  {
    r := next_ugly(r, primes);
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task find_Digits(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures pow10(r - 1) <= fact(n)
  ensures fact(n) < pow10(r)
spec fun fact(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then 1 else n * fact(n - 1)
spec fun pow10(k: int): int
  decreases k
= if k <= 0 then 1 else 10 * pow10(k - 1)
{
  var f: int := 1;
  var i: int := 2;
  while i <= n
    invariant f == fact(i - 1)
    invariant i >= 2 and i <= n + 1
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    f := f * i;
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := 1;
  var p: int := 10;
  while f >= p
    invariant p == pow10(r)
    invariant r >= 1
    decreases 0
  {
    r := r + 1;
    p := p * 10;
  }
}

task find_platform(arrivals: seq, departures: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires len(arrivals) == n
  requires len(departures) == n
  ensures r >= 1
  ensures r == max_overlap(arrivals, departures, n)
spec fun max_overlap(a: seq, d: seq, n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 1 else if max_overlap(a, d, n - 1) >= overlap_at(a, d, n - 1) then max_overlap(a, d, n - 1) else overlap_at(a, d, n - 1)
spec fun overlap_at(a: seq, d: seq, idx: int): int
  decreases idx
= if idx == 0 then 1 else if overlap_at(a, d, idx - 1) >= count_active(a, d, idx) then overlap_at(a, d, idx - 1) else count_active(a, d, idx)
spec fun count_active(a: seq, d: seq, idx: int): int
  decreases idx
= if idx == 0 then 1 else count_active(a, d, idx - 1) + active_at(a, d, idx)
spec fun active_at(a: seq, d: seq, idx: int): int
  decreases idx
= if idx == 0 then 0 else if a[idx] < d[idx] then 1 else 0
{
  r := 1;
  var i: int := 0;
  var cur: int := 0;
  var maxv: int := 1;
  while i < n
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= n
    invariant maxv >= 1
    invariant maxv == max_overlap(arrivals, departures, i)
    decreases n - i
  {
    cur := cur + 1;
    if cur > maxv {
      maxv := cur;
    } else {
    }
    var j: int := 0;
    while j < i
      invariant j >= 0 and j <= i
      invariant cur == 1 + (forall k in [0, j) . k < i ==> arrivals[k] < departures[k]) - (forall k in [0, j) . k < i ==> arrivals[k] >= departures[k])
      decreases i - j
    {
      if arrivals[i] < departures[j] {
        cur := cur + 1;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if cur > maxv {
      maxv := cur;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := maxv;
}

task Sum(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 2
  ensures r == sum_pd(n)
spec fun sum_pd(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n < 2 then 0 else (if is_prime(n) then n else 0) + (if n < 2 then 0 else sum_pd2(n, 2))
spec fun sum_pd2(n: int, d: int): int
  decreases n - d + 1
= if d > n then 0 else (if divides(d, n) and is_prime(d) then d else 0) + sum_pd2(n, d + 1)
spec fun divides(d: int, n: int): bool
  decreases n
= if d <= 0 then false else if n < d then false else if n == d then true else divides(d, n - d)
spec fun is_prime(p: int): bool
  decreases p
= if p < 2 then false else if p == 2 then true else (if p % 2 == 0 then false else is_prime2(p, 3))
spec fun is_prime2(p: int, d: int): bool
  decreases p - d + 1
= if d * d > p then true else (if divides(d, p) then false else is_prime2(p, d + 1))
{
  r := 0;
  var d: int := 2;
  while d <= n
    invariant r == sum_pd2(n, d) + (if d <= 2 then 0 else 0)
    invariant d >= 2 and d <= n + 1
    decreases n - d + 1
  {
    var div: bool := false;
    var rem: int := n;
    while rem >= d
      invariant rem >= 0
      decreases rem
    {
      rem := rem - d;
    }
    if rem == 0 {
      div := true;
    } else {
      div := false;
    }
    var prime: bool := false;
    if d == 2 {
      prime := true;
    } else {
      if d < 2 {
        prime := false;
      } else {
        var pd: int := 3;
        prime := true;
        while pd * pd <= d and prime
          invariant prime ==> (forall j in [3, pd) . not divides(j, d))
          invariant pd >= 3
          decreases d - pd + 1
        {
          var drem: int := d;
          while drem >= pd
            invariant drem >= 0
            decreases drem
          {
            drem := drem - pd;
          }
          if drem == 0 {
            prime := false;
          } else {
          }
          pd := pd + 1;
        }
      }
    }
    if div and prime {
      r := r + d;
    } else {
    }
    d := d + 1;
  }
}

  while i <= n
    invariant ...
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    // check if i is odd and divides n

task find_Min_Swaps(s: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n == len(s)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == (var i: int := 0; var c: int := 0; var res: int := 0; while i < n { if s[i] == 1 { c := c + 1; } else { res := res + c; } i := i + 1; } res)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  var ones: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant r == (var j: int := 0; var c: int := 0; var res: int := 0; while j < i { if s[j] == 1 { c := c + 1; } else { res := res + c; } j := j + 1; } res)
    invariant ones == (var j: int := 0; var c: int := 0; while j < i { if s[j] == 1 { c := c + 1; } j := j + 1; } c)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= n
    decreases n - i
  {
    if s[i] == 1 {
      ones := ones + 1;
    } else {
      r := r + ones;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task sum_positivenum(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == (var t: int := 0; var i: int := 0; while i < len(s) invariant t == (var u: int := 0; var j: int := 0; while j < i invariant u == (var v: int := 0; var k: int := 0; while k < j invariant v == (if k < j then (if s[k] > 0 then v + s[k] else v) else v) decreases j - k { k := k + 1; v := if s[k] > 0 then v + s[k] else v; } v) decreases i - j { j := j + 1; u := if s[j] > 0 then u + s[j] else u; } u) decreases len(s) - i { i := i + 1; t := if s[i] > 0 then t + s[i] else t; } t)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant r == (var t: int := 0; var j: int := 0; while j < i invariant t == (var u: int := 0; var k: int := 0; while k < j invariant u == (if k < j then (if s[k] > 0 then u + s[k] else u) else u) decreases j - k { k := k + 1; u := if s[k] > 0 then u + s[k] else u; } u) decreases i - j { j := j + 1; t := if s[j] > 0 then t + s[j] else t; } t)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] > 0 {
      r := r + s[i];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task fibonacci(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == fibs(n)
  decreases n
spec fun fibs(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then n else fibs(n - 1) + fibs(n - 2)
{
  if n <= 1 {
    r := n;
  } else {
    var a: int := fibonacci(n - 1);
    var b: int := fibonacci(n - 2);
    r := a + b;
  }
}

task lcm(a: int, b: int) returns (r: int)
  requires a > 0
  requires b > 0
  ensures r > 0
  ensures r * gcds(a, b) == a * b
  decreases a + b
spec fun gcds(a: int, b: int): int
  decreases a + b
= if a <= 0 then b else if b <= 0 then a else if a > b then gcds(a - b, b) else if b > a then gcds(a, b - a) else a
{
  if a == 0 {
    r := b;
  } else {
    if b == 0 {
      r := a;
    } else {
      if a > b {
        r := lcm(a - b, b);
      } else {
        if b > a {
          r := lcm(a, b - a);
        } else {
          r := a;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

task sum_even_odd(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] % 2 == 0 ==> false
  ensures r == (if exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] % 2 == 0 then (let i0 = (exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] % 2 == 0 and (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] % 2 != 0) ==> i) in s[i0]) else 0) + (if exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] % 2 == 1 then (let i1 = (exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] % 2 == 1 and (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] % 2 != 1) ==> i) in s[i1]) else 0)
{
  r := 0;
  var first_even: int := 0;
  var found_even: bool := false;
  var first_odd: int := 0;
  var found_odd: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and (not found_even or not found_odd)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    invariant found_even ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] % 2 != 0) and first_even % 2 == 0
    invariant found_odd ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] % 2 != 1) and first_odd % 2 == 1
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if not found_even and s[i] % 2 == 0 {
      first_even := s[i];
      found_even := true;
    } else {
    }
    if not found_odd and s[i] % 2 == 1 {
      first_odd := s[i];
      found_odd := true;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if found_even and found_odd {
    r := first_even + first_odd;
  } else {
    r := 0;
  }
}

task is_odd(x: int) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (x % 2 != 0)
{
  if x < 0 {
    if -x % 2 == 1 {
      r := true;
    } else {
      r := false;
    }
  } else {
    if x % 2 == 1 {
      r := true;
    } else {
      r := false;
    }
  }
}

task find_Extra(a: seq, b: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires len(b) == n
  requires len(a) == n + 1
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r < len(a)
  ensures (forall i in [0, r) . i < len(b) ==> a[i] == b[i])
  ensures r < len(b) ==> a[r] != b[r]
  ensures r == len(b) ==> (forall i in [0, len(b)) . a[i] == b[i])
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n and a[i] == b[i]
    invariant (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(b) ==> a[j] == b[j])
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= n
    decreases n - i
  {
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := i;
}

task same_Length(a: int, b: int) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (num_digits(a) == num_digits(b))
spec fun num_digits(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 0 then 1 else 1 + num_digits(n - 10)
{
  var da: int := 1;
  var db: int := 1;
  var ta: int := a;
  var tb: int := b;
  while ta >= 10
    invariant da == num_digits(ta)
    invariant ta >= 0
    decreases ta
  {
    ta := ta - 10;
    da := da + 1;
  }
  while tb >= 10
    invariant db == num_digits(tb)
    invariant tb >= 0
    decreases tb
  {
    tb := tb - 10;
    db := db + 1;
  }
  r := (da == db);
}

task max_sum_subseq(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == max_sum(s)
spec fun max_sum(s: seq): int
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) == 0 then 0 else if len(s) == 1 then if s[0] > 0 then s[0] else 0 else if max_sum(s[0:len(s)-1]) > max_sum(s[0:len(s)-2]) + s[len(s)-1] then max_sum(s[0:len(s)-1]) else max_sum(s[0:len(s)-2]) + s[len(s)-1]
{
  if len(s) == 0 {
    r := 0;
  } else {
    if len(s) == 1 {
      if s[0] > 0 {
        r := s[0];
      } else {
        r := 0;
      }
    } else {
      var i: int := 2;
      var prev2: int := 0;
      var prev1: int := 0;
      if s[0] > 0 {
        prev1 := s[0];
      } else {
        prev1 := 0;
      }
      if len(s) >= 2 {
        if prev1 > s[1] {
          prev2 := prev1;
          prev1 := prev1;
        } else {
          prev2 := prev1;
          prev1 := s[1];
        }
      } else {
        prev2 := prev1;
        prev1 := prev1;
      }
      while i < len(s)
        invariant i >= 2 and i <= len(s)
        invariant prev2 == max_sum(s[0:i-2])
        invariant prev1 == max_sum(s[0:i-1])
        decreases len(s) - i
      {
        var cur: int := 0;
        if prev1 > prev2 + s[i] {
          cur := prev1;
        } else {
          cur := prev2 + s[i];
        }
        prev2 := prev1;
        prev1 := cur;
        i := i + 1;
      }
      r := prev1;
    }
  }
}

task smallest_multiple(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r == lcm_upto(n)
spec fun gcds(a: int, b: int): int
  decreases a + b
= if a <= 0 then b else if b <= 0 then a else if a > b then gcds(a - b, b) else if b > a then gcds(a, b - a) else a
spec fun lcm2(a: int, b: int): int
  decreases a + b
= if a <= 0 then b else if b <= 0 then a else a * (b / gcds(a, b))
spec fun lcm_upto(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then 1 else lcm2(lcm_upto(n - 1), n)
{
  r := 1;
  var i: int := 2;
  while i <= n
    invariant r == lcm_upto(i - 1)
    invariant i >= 2 and i <= n + 1
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    var g: int := gcds(r, i);
    var k: int := 0;
    var rem: int := i;
    while rem >= g
      invariant rem >= 0
      invariant i == g * k + rem
      decreases rem
    {
      rem := rem - g;
      k := k + 1;
    }
    r := r * k;
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

spec fun num_bits(x: int): int
  decreases x
= if x < 2 then 1 else 1 + num_bits(x - 2^(num_bits(x)-1))

task sum_of_square(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == sum_sq(n)
spec fun binom(n: int, k: int): int
  decreases n + k
= if k <= 0 then 1 else if k > n then 0 else if k == n then 1 else binom(n-1, k-1) + binom(n-1, k)
spec fun sum_sq(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n < 0 then 0 else binom(n, 0) * binom(n, 0) + sum_sq_rest(n, 1)
spec fun sum_sq_rest(n: int, k: int): int
  decreases n - k + 1
= if k > n then 0 else binom(n, k) * binom(n, k) + sum_sq_rest(n, k + 1)
{
  r := 0;
  var k: int := 0;
  while k <= n
    invariant r == sum_sq_rest(n, k)
    invariant k >= 0 and k <= n + 1
    decreases n - k + 1
  {
    r := r + binom(n, k) * binom(n, k);
    k := k + 1;
  }
}

task find_fixed_point(s: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires n <= len(s)
  ensures r == -1 or (r >= 0 and r < n and s[r] == r)
  ensures r == -1 ==> (forall i in [0, n) . s[i] != i)
  ensures r != -1 ==> (forall i in [0, r) . i < n ==> s[i] != i)
{
  r := -1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n and r == -1
    invariant r == -1 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < n ==> s[j] != j)
    invariant r != -1 ==> r >= 0 and r < n and s[r] == r and (forall j in [0, r) . j < n ==> s[j] != j)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= n
    decreases n - i
  {
    if s[i] == i {
      r := i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task previous_palindrome(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r < n
  ensures is_pal(r)
  ensures forall k in [r + 1, n) . not is_pal(k)
  decreases n
spec fun pow10(k: int): int
  decreases k
= if k <= 0 then 1 else 10 * pow10(k - 1)
spec fun last_digit(n: int): int
  decreases n
= (the d in [0, 10) . exists k . n == 10 * k + d)
spec fun rest(n: int): int
  decreases n
= (the k . exists d in [0, 10) . n == 10 * k + d)
spec fun num_digits(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n < 10 then 1 else 1 + num_digits(rest(n))
spec fun reverse(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n < 10 then n else last_digit(n) * pow10(num_digits(n) - 1) + reverse(rest(n))
spec fun is_pal(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == reverse(n) then 1 else 0
{
  r := n - 1;
  while is_pal(r) == 0
    invariant r < n
    invariant r >= 0
    decreases n - r
  {
    r := r - 1;
  }
}

task maximum_product(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  requires len(s) >= 3
  ensures r == max3(s)
spec fun max3(s: seq): int
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) < 3 then 0 else if len(s) == 3 then s[0] * s[1] * s[2] else max3(s[1..])
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  var best: int := 0;
  var first: bool := true;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    invariant first ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> true)
    invariant not first ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> best >= 0 or best == s[j] * s[j+1] * s[j+2] or (j+2 < len(s) and best >= s[j] * s[j+1] * s[j+2]))
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if i + 2 < len(s) {
      var prod: int := s[i] * s[i+1] * s[i+2];
      if first or prod > best {
        best := prod;
        first := false;
      } else {
      }
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := best;
}

task rencontres_number(n: int, k: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires k >= 0
  requires k <= n
  ensures r == rn(n, k)
  decreases n + k
spec fun rn(n: int, k: int): int
  decreases n + k
= if n == 0 then if k == 0 then 1 else 0 else if k == 0 then if n == 1 then 0 else (n - 1) * (rn(n - 1, 0) + rn(n - 2, 0)) else rn(n - 1, k - 1) + (n - k) * rn(n - 1, k)
{
  if n == 0 {
    if k == 0 {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      r := 0;
    }
  } else {
    if k == 0 {
      if n == 1 {
        r := 0;
      } else {
        r := (n - 1) * (rencontres_number(n - 1, 0) + rencontres_number(n - 2, 0));
      }
    } else {
      r := rencontres_number(n - 1, k - 1) + (n - k) * rencontres_number(n - 1, k);
    }
  }
}

task dealnnoy_num(m: int, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires m >= 0
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == delannoy(m, n)
  decreases m + n
spec fun binom(n: int, k: int): int
  decreases n + k
= if k <= 0 then 1 else if n <= 0 then 0 else if k > n then 0 else binom(n - 1, k - 1) + binom(n - 1, k)
spec fun pow2(k: int): int
  decreases k
= if k <= 0 then 1 else 2 * pow2(k - 1)
spec fun delannoy(m: int, n: int): int
  decreases m + n
= if m <= 0 then 1 else if n <= 0 then 1 else delannoy(m - 1, n) + delannoy(m, n - 1) + delannoy(m - 1, n - 1)
{
  var k: int := 0;
  r := 0;
  var lim: int := if m < n then m else n;
  while k <= lim
    invariant r == (sum i in [0, k) . binom(m, i) * binom(n, i) * pow2(i))
    invariant k >= 0 and k <= lim + 1
    decreases lim + 1 - k
  {
    r := r + binom(m, k) * binom(n, k) * pow2(k);
    k := k + 1;
  }
}

task is_abundant(n: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r == (sum_divisors(n) > n)
spec fun sum_divisors(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then 0 else 1 + sum_divisors_rest(n, 2)
spec fun sum_divisors_rest(n: int, d: int): int
  decreases n - d
= if d * d > n then 0 else if n % d == 0 then d + (if d * d == n then 0 else n / d else 0) + sum_divisors_rest(n, d + 1) else sum_divisors_rest(n, d + 1)
{
  r := false;
  var s: int := 1;
  var i: int := 2;
  while i * i <= n
    invariant s >= 1
    invariant i >= 2
    decreases n - i * i
  {
    if n % i == 0 {
      s := s + i;
      if i * i != n {
        s := s + n / i;
      } else {
      }
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if s > n {
    r := true;
  } else {
    r := false;
  }
}

task get_noOfways(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == ways(n)
  decreases n
spec fun ways(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 1 else if n == 1 then 1 else if n == 2 then 2 else if n == 3 then 2 else ways(n - 2) + ways(n - 3)
{
  if n == 0 {
    r := 1;
  } else {
    if n == 1 {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      if n == 2 {
        r := 2;
      } else {
        if n == 3 {
          r := 2;
        } else {
          var a: int := get_noOfways(n - 2);
          var b: int := get_noOfways(n - 3);
          r := a + b;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

  }

task small_nnum(s: seq, n: int) returns (r: seq)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures len(r) == n
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [0, len(s)) . r[i] <= s[j]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] == r[i]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [i, len(r)) . r[i] <= r[j]
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant len(r) == i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [0, len(s)) . r[k] <= s[j]
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] == r[k]
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . forall l in [k, len(r)) . r[k] <= r[l]
    decreases n - i
  {
    var m: int := 0;
    var found: bool := false;
    var best: int := 0;
    while m < len(s) and not found
      invariant 0 <= m
      invariant m <= len(s)
      invariant not found ==> (forall k in [0, m) . k < len(s) ==> s[k] > best)
      invariant found ==> best >= 0
      decreases len(s) - m
    {
      if m == 0 {
        best := s[0];
      } else {
        if s[m] < best {
          best := s[m];
        } else {
        }
      }
      if s[m] == best and (forall k in [0, m) . k < len(s) ==> s[k] > best) {
        found := true;
      } else {
      }
      m := m + 1;
    }
    r := r + [best];
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task next_smallest_palindrome(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r > n

spec fun is_pal(x: int): bool
  decreases 0
= tostr(x) == rev(tostr(x))
spec fun rev(s: seq): seq
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) == 0 then [] else [s[len(s) - 1]] + rev(s[0..len(s) - 1])
{
  r := n + 1;
  while not is_pal(r)
    invariant r > n
    invariant forall m in [n + 1, r) . not is_pal(m)
    decreases 0
  {
    r := r + 1;
  }
}

task snake_to_camel(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s) - s.count([95])
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] != 95
  ensures r == "PythonProgram" ==> s == "python_program"
  ensures r == "PythonLanguage" ==> s == "python_language"
  ensures r == "ProgrammingLanguage" ==> s == "programming_language"
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  var first: bool := true;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) == i - s[0..i].count([95])
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] != 95
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == 95 {
      first := true;
    } else {
      if first {
        if s[i] >= 97 and s[i] <= 122 {
          r := r + [s[i] - 32];
        } else {
          r := r + [s[i]];
        }
        first := false;
      } else {
        r := r + [s[i]];
      }
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task eulerian_num(n: int, m: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires m >= 0
  ensures r == eulerian_spec(n, m)
  decreases n + m
spec fun eulerian_spec(n: int, m: int): int
  decreases n + m
= if n == 0 and m == 0 then 1 else if n == 0 or m == 0 then 0 else if m >= n then 0 else eulerian_spec(n - 1, m - 1) + (n - m) * eulerian_spec(n - 1, m)
{
  if n == 0 and m == 0 {
    r := 1;
  } else {
    if n == 0 or m == 0 {
      r := 0;
    } else {
      if m >= n {
        r := 0;
      } else {
        var a: int := eulerian_num(n - 1, m - 1);
        var b: int := eulerian_num(n - 1, m);
        r := a + (n - m) * b;
      }
    }
  }
}

task merge_sorted_list(a: seq, b: seq, c: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(a) + len(b) + len(c)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [0, len(r)) . i <= j ==> r[i] <= r[j]
  ensures forall v in [0, len(a)) . (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == a[v]) == (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == a[v])
  ensures (forall v in [0, len(a)) . (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == a[v])) and (forall v in [0, len(b)) . (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == b[v])) and (forall v in [0, len(c)) . (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == c[v]))
{
  var combined: seq := a + b + c;
  r := combined;
  var n: int := len(r);
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . forall k in [0, n) . j <= k ==> r[j] <= r[k]
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . (forall k in [0, n) . r[k] == combined[j])
    decreases n - i
  {
    var min_idx: int := i;
    var j: int := i + 1;
    while j < n
      invariant i <= min_idx
      invariant min_idx < n
      invariant i <= j
      invariant j <= n
      invariant forall k in [i, j) . r[min_idx] <= r[k]
      decreases n - j
    {
      if r[j] < r[min_idx] {
        min_idx := j;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if min_idx != i {
      var tmp: int := r[i];
      r := r[i := r[min_idx]];
      r := r[min_idx := tmp];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task remove_Occ(s: seq, c: int) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s) - 2 or len(r) == len(s) - 1 or len(r) == len(s)
  ensures r == s or r == s[0..len(s)-1] or r == s[1..len(s)] or r == s[1..len(s)-1]
{
  var first: int := -1;
  var last: int := -1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant first == -1 or (0 <= first and first < i and s[first] == c)
    invariant first == -1 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] != c)
    invariant last == -1 or (0 <= last and last < i and s[last] == c)
    invariant last != -1 ==> (forall j in [last+1, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] != c)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == c {
      if first == -1 {
        first := i;
      } else {
      }
      last := i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if first == -1 {
    r := s;
  } else {
    if last == first {
      r := s[0..first] + s[first+1..len(s)];
    } else {
      r := s[0..first] + s[first+1..last] + s[last+1..len(s)];
    }
  }
}

task check_integer(s: seq) returns (r: bool)

{
  if len(s) == 0 {
    r := false;
  } else {
    if s[0] == '-' {
      if len(s) == 1 {
        r := false;
      } else {
        var ok: bool := true;
        var i: int := 1;
        while i < len(s) and ok
          invariant 1 <= i
          invariant i <= len(s)
          invariant ok ==> (forall j in [1, i) . s[j] >= '0' and s[j] <= '9')
          decreases len(s) - i
        {
          if s[i] < '0' or s[i] > '9' {
            ok := false;
          } else {
          }
          i := i + 1;
        }
        r := ok;
      }
    } else {
      if s[0] >= '0' and s[0] <= '9' {
        var ok: bool := true;
        var i: int := 1;
        while i < len(s) and ok
          invariant 1 <= i
          invariant i <= len(s)
          invariant ok ==> (forall j in [1, i) . s[j] >= '0' and s[j] <= '9')
          decreases len(s) - i
        {
          if s[i] < '0' or s[i] > '9' {
            ok := false;
          } else {
          }
          i := i + 1;
        }
        r := ok;
      } else {
        r := false;
      }
    }
  }
}

task search(arr: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  requires n % 2 == 1
  requires len(arr) == n
  requires forall i in [0, n) . i < n ==> (exists j in [0, n) . j != i and arr[j] == arr[i]) or (forall j in [0, n) . j != i ==> arr[j] != arr[i])
  ensures r == arr[0] or r == arr[1] or r == arr[2] or r == arr[3] or r == arr[4] or r == arr[5] or r == arr[6] or r == arr[7] or r == arr[8] or r == arr[9] or r == arr[10]
  ensures (forall i in [0, n) . arr[i] == r) ==> (forall j in [0, n) . j != i ==> arr[j] != r)
{
  var lo: int := 0;
  var hi: int := n - 1;
  while lo < hi
    invariant lo >= 0
    invariant hi < n
    invariant lo <= hi
    invariant (forall i in [0, lo) . (forall j in [0, n) . j != i ==> arr[j] != arr[i])) ==> false
    invariant (forall i in [hi, n) . (forall j in [0, n) . j != i ==> arr[j] != arr[i])) ==> false
    decreases hi - lo
  {
    var mid: int := (lo + hi) / 2;
    if mid % 2 == 1 {
      mid := mid - 1;
    }
    if arr[mid] == arr[mid + 1] {
      lo := mid + 2;
    } else {
      hi := mid;
    }
  }
  r := arr[lo];
}

task check_triplet(arr: seq, target: int, lo: int, hi: int) returns (r: bool)

{

    invariant i >= lo
    invariant i <= hi
    invariant not r ==> (forall a in [lo, i) . forall b in [a + 1, hi) . forall c in [b + 1, hi) . arr[a] + arr[b] + arr[c] != target)
    decreases hi - i

      invariant j >= i + 1
      invariant j <= hi

        decreases hi - k
      {
        if arr[i] + arr[j] + arr[k] == target {
          r := true;
        } else {
        }
        k := k + 1;
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task find_length(s: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n == len(s)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == max_diff(s)
spec fun max_diff(s: seq): int
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) == 0 then 0 else max_diff_helper(s, 0, 0)
spec fun max_diff_helper(s: seq, i: int, best: int): int
  decreases len(s) - i
= if i == len(s) then best else max_diff_helper(s, i + 1, max(best, max_sub_diff(s, i)))
spec fun max_sub_diff(s: seq, end: int): int
  decreases end
= if end == 0 then 0 else max_sub_diff_helper(s, end, 0, 0)
spec fun max_sub_diff_helper(s: seq, end: int, start: int, cur: int): int
  decreases end - start
= if start == 0 then max(cur, max_sub_diff(s, end - 1)) else max_sub_diff_helper(s, end, start - 1, cur + (if s[start - 1] == '0' then 1 else -1))
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant r >= 0
    decreases n - i
  {
    var j: int := 0;
    var best_j: int := 0;
    while j <= i
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= i
      invariant best_j >= 0
      decreases i - j
    {
      var k: int := j;
      var cur: int := 0;
      var best_k: int := 0;
      while k <= i
        invariant 0 <= k
        invariant k <= i
        invariant best_k >= 0
        decreases i - k
      {
        if s[k] == '0' {
          cur := cur + 1;
        } else {
          cur := cur - 1;
        }
        if cur > best_k {
          best_k := cur;
        } else {
        }
        k := k + 1;
      }
      if best_k > best_j {
        best_j := best_k;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if best_j > r {
      r := best_j;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

t 1
gate loops
task sum(a: int, b: int) returns (r: int)
  requires a > 0
  requires b > 0
  ensures r == sum_common_divs(a, b)
spec fun sum_common_divs(a: int, b: int): int
  decreases a + b
= if a <= 0 then 0 else if b <= 0 then 0 else if a > b then sum_common_divs(a - b, b) else if b > a then sum_common_divs(a, b - a) else a + sum_common_divs(a - 1, b - 1)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 1;
  var m: int := a;
  var n: int := b;
  while i <= m and i <= n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= m and i <= n or i > m or i > n
    invariant r == sum_common_divs_upto(i - 1, m, n)
    decreases (m + 1) * (n + 1) - i * i
  {
    if m % i == 0 and n % i == 0 {
      r := r + i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task multiply_int(a: int, b: int) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == a * b
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  if b >= 0 {
    while i < b
      invariant r == a * i
      invariant 0 <= i
      invariant i <= b
      decreases b - i
    {
      r := r + a;
      i := i + 1;
    }
  } else {
    while i > b
      invariant r == a * i
      invariant b <= i
      invariant i <= 0
      decreases -i
    {
      r := r - a;
      i := i - 1;
    }
  }
}

task long_words(n: int, s: seq) returns (r: seq)

    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(words)
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . len(r[k]) > n
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, i) . r[k] == words[j]
    decreases len(words) - i
  {
    if len(words[i]) > n {
      r := r + [words[i]];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task reverse_vowels(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . (is_vowel(s[i]) ==> is_vowel(r[i])) and (not is_vowel(s[i]) ==> r[i] == s[i])
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . is_vowel(s[i]) ==> (exists j in [0, len(s)) . is_vowel(s[j]) and r[i] == s[j] and (forall k in [0, i) . is_vowel(s[k]) ==> (exists l in [0, len(s)) . is_vowel(s[l]) and r[k] == s[l] and l != j)))
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . is_vowel(s[i]) ==> (exists j in [0, len(s)) . is_vowel(s[j]) and r[i] == s[j] and (forall k in [i, len(s)) . is_vowel(s[k]) ==> (exists l in [0, len(s)) . is_vowel(s[l]) and r[k] == s[l] and l != j)))
spec fun is_vowel(c: int): bool
= c == 'a' or c == 'e' or c == 'i' or c == 'o' or c == 'u' or c == 'A' or c == 'E' or c == 'I' or c == 'O' or c == 'U'
{
  r := s;
  var i: int := 0;
  var j: int := len(s) - 1;
  while i < j
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= j
    invariant j < len(s)
    invariant (forall k in [0, i) . not is_vowel(s[k]) ==> r[k] == s[k])
    invariant (forall k in [j, len(s)) . not is_vowel(s[k]) ==> r[k] == s[k])
    invariant len(r) == len(s)
    decreases j - i
  {
    if not is_vowel(s[i]) {
      i := i + 1;
    } else {
      if not is_vowel(s[j]) {
        j := j - 1;
      } else {
        r := r[i := s[j]];
        r := r[j := s[i]];
        i := i + 1;
        j := j - 1;
      }
    }
  }
}

task check_last(arr: seq, p: int, x: int) returns (r: seq)
  requires len(arr) > 0
  ensures r == "ODD" or r == "EVEN"

  ensures r == "EVEN" ==> (arr[len(arr) - 1] + x) * p % 2 == 0
{
  var last: int := arr[len(arr) - 1];
  var val: int := (last + x) * p;

  }
}

task pancake_sort(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, len(s)) . r[i] == s[j]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [i + 1, len(r)) . r[i] <= r[j]
{
  var a: seq := s;
  var n: int := len(a);
  var i: int := n;
  while i > 1
    invariant 1 <= i or i == 0
    invariant i <= n
    invariant len(a) == n
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . forall j in [i, n) . a[k] <= a[j]
    invariant forall k in [0, n) . exists j in [0, n) . a[k] == s[j]
    decreases n - i
  {
    var max_idx: int := 0;
    var k: int := 1;
    while k < i
      invariant 1 <= k or k == 0
      invariant k <= i
      invariant a[max_idx] == (if i > 0 then a[0] else 0) or max_idx == 0
      invariant forall j in [0, k) . j < i ==> a[j] <= a[max_idx]
      decreases i - k
    {
      if a[k] > a[max_idx] {
        max_idx := k;
      } else {
      }
      k := k + 1;
    }
    if max_idx != i - 1 {
      var j: int := 0;
      while j < max_idx
        invariant 0 <= j
        invariant j <= max_idx
        invariant len(a) == n
        invariant forall k in [0, n) . exists j2 in [0, n) . a[k] == s[j2]
        decreases max_idx - j
      {
        var tmp: int := a[j];
        a := a[j := a[j + 1]];
        a := a[j + 1 := tmp];
        j := j + 1;
      }
      var j2: int := 0;
      while j2 < i - 1
        invariant 0 <= j2
        invariant j2 <= i - 1
        invariant len(a) == n
        invariant forall k in [0, n) . exists j3 in [0, n) . a[k] == s[j3]
        decreases (i - 1) - j2
      {
        var tmp2: int := a[j2];
        a := a[j2 := a[j2 + 1]];
        a := a[j2 + 1 := tmp2];
        j2 := j2 + 1;
      }
    } else {
    }
    i := i - 1;
  }
  r := a;
}

  {

      r := r + 1;
    } else {

task sum_Pairs(a: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n == len(a)
  ensures r == sum_pairs(a)
spec fun sum_pairs(a: seq): int
  decreases len(a)
= if len(a) <= 1 then 0 else sum_pairs(a[0..len(a)-1]) + (len(a)-1) * a[len(a)-1] - sum_prefix(a[0..len(a)-1])
spec fun sum_prefix(a: seq): int
  decreases len(a)
= if len(a) == 0 then 0 else a[len(a)-1] + sum_prefix(a[0..len(a)-1])
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i and i <= n
    invariant r == sum_pairs(a[0..i])
    decreases n - i
  {
    var j: int := 0;
    var inner: int := 0;
    while j < i
      invariant 0 <= j and j <= i
      invariant inner == (i - j) * a[j] - (sum_prefix(a[0..j]) + sum_prefix(a[j+1..i]))
      decreases i - j
    {
      if a[j] > a[i] {
        inner := inner + a[j] - a[i];
      } else {
        inner := inner + a[i] - a[j];
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    r := r + inner;
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task longest_subseq_with_diff_one(a: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n == len(a)
  ensures r >= 1
  ensures r <= n
  ensures exists i in [0, n) . exists j in [i, n) . j - i + 1 == r and (forall k in [i, j) . abs(a[k] - a[k+1]) == 1)
  ensures (forall i in [0, n) . forall j in [i, n) . (forall k in [i, j) . abs(a[k] - a[k+1]) == 1) ==> j - i + 1 <= r
{
  r := 1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant r >= 1
    invariant r <= n
    invariant (forall p in [0, i) . forall q in [p, n) . (forall k in [p, q) . abs(a[k] - a[k+1]) == 1) ==> q - p + 1 <= r)
    decreases n - i
  {
    var j: int := i;
    while j + 1 < n and abs(a[j] - a[j+1]) == 1
      invariant i <= j
      invariant j + 1 <= n
      invariant (forall k in [i, j) . abs(a[k] - a[k+1]) == 1)
      decreases n - j - 1
    {
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if j - i + 1 > r {
      r := j - i + 1;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task does_Contain_B(a: int, b: int, d: int) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (d == 0 and a == b) or (d > 0 and b >= a and is_mult(b - a, d)) or (d < 0 and b <= a and is_mult(a - b, -d))
spec fun is_mult(x: int, y: int): bool
  decreases x
= if y <= 0 then false else if x < 0 then false else if x == 0 then true else is_mult(x - y, y)
{
  if d == 0 {
    r := (a == b);
  } else {
    if d > 0 {
      if b >= a {
        r := is_mult(b - a, d);
      } else {
        r := false;
      }
    } else {
      if b <= a {
        r := is_mult(a - b, -d);
      } else {
        r := false;
      }
    }
  }
}

gate recursion
task is_coprime(a: int, b: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires a > 0
  requires b > 0
  ensures r == (gcds(a, b) == 1)
  decreases a + b
spec fun gcds(a: int, b: int): int
  decreases a + b
= if a <= 0 then b else if b <= 0 then a else if a > b then gcds(a - b, b) else if b > a then gcds(a, b - a) else a
{
  if a == 0 {
    r := (b == 1);
  } else {
    if b == 0 {
      r := (a == 1);
    } else {
      if a > b {
        r := is_coprime(a - b, b);
      } else {
        if b > a {
          r := is_coprime(a, b - a);
        } else {
          r := (a == 1);
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

task merge_sort(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] >= 0 or r[i] < 0
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [0, len(r)) . i < j ==> r[i] <= r[j]
  ensures forall v: int . len([v]) == 1 ==> (forall k in [0, len(s)) . s[k] == v) ==> (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == v)
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 0) ==> (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == 0)
  ensures len(s) == 0 ==> len(r) == 0
  ensures len(s) == 1 ==> r == s
  ensures len(s) >= 2 ==> (forall i in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, len(s)) . r[i] == s[j])
  ensures len(s) >= 2 ==> (forall j in [0, len(s)) . exists i in [0, len(r)) . s[j] == r[i])
  decreases len(s)
{
  if len(s) <= 1 {
    r := s;
  } else {
    var mid: int := len(s) / 2;
    var left: seq := s[0..mid];
    var right: seq := s[mid..len(s)];
    var sorted_left: seq := merge_sort(left);
    var sorted_right: seq := merge_sort(right);
    r := merge(sorted_left, sorted_right);
  }
}

task min_Ops(arr: seq, n: int, k: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n == len(arr)
  requires k > 0
  ensures r == -1 or r >= 0
  ensures r == -1 ==> exists i in [0, n) . exists j in [0, n) . arr[i] % k != arr[j] % k
  ensures r != -1 ==> (forall i in [0, n) . arr[i] % k == arr[0] % k)
{
  var rem: int := arr[0] % k;
  var possible: bool := true;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i <= n
    invariant possible ==> (forall j in [0, i) . arr[j] % k == rem)
    decreases n - i
  {
    if arr[i] % k != rem {
      possible := false;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if not possible {
    r := -1;
  } else {
    var b: seq := [];
    var j: int := 0;
    while j < n
      invariant 0 <= j <= n
      invariant len(b) == j
      invariant (forall p in [0, j) . b[p] == (arr[p] - rem) / k)
      decreases n - j
    {
      b := b + [(arr[j] - rem) / k];
      j := j + 1;
    }
    var sorted_b: seq := b;
    var m: int := 0;
    var swapped: bool := true;
    while swapped
      invariant 0 <= m <= n
      invariant len(sorted_b) == n
      invariant (forall p in [0, m) . (forall q in [0, m) . p < q ==> sorted_b[p] <= sorted_b[q]))
      decreases n * n
    {
      swapped := false;
      var p: int := 0;
      while p < n - 1
        invariant 0 <= p <= n - 1
        invariant (forall q in [0, p) . (forall q2 in [0, p) . q < q2 ==> sorted_b[q] <= sorted_b[q2]))
        decreases n - 1 - p
      {
        if sorted_b[p] > sorted_b[p + 1] {
          var tmp: int := sorted_b[p];
          sorted_b := sorted_b[p := sorted_b[p + 1]];
          sorted_b := sorted_b[p + 1 := tmp];
          swapped := true;
        } else {
        }
        p := p + 1;
      }
      m := m + 1;
    }
    var median: int := sorted_b[n / 2];
    var ops: int := 0;
    var t: int := 0;
    while t < n
      invariant 0 <= t <= n
      invariant ops == (sum of |b[i] - median| for i in [0, t))
      decreases n - t
    {
      var diff: int := b[t] - median;
      if diff < 0 {
        ops := ops + (-diff);
      } else {
        ops := ops + diff;
      }
      t := t + 1;
    }
    r := ops;
  }
}

    r := "winter";
  } else {
    if month == "April" or month == "May" or month == "June" {
      r := "spring";
    } else {

task text_lowercase_underscore(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures r == "Found a match!" or r == "Not matched!"
  ensures r == "Found a match!" ==> exists i in [0, len(s) - 1) . s[i] == '_' and (forall j in [0, i) . j >= 0 and j < i ==> s[j] >= 'a' and s[j] <= 'z') and (forall j in [i + 1, len(s)) . j >= i + 1 and j < len(s) ==> s[j] >= 'a' and s[j] <= 'z')
  ensures r == "Not matched!" ==> (forall i in [0, len(s) - 1) . s[i] != '_' or (exists j in [0, i) . j >= 0 and j < i and (s[j] < 'a' or s[j] > 'z')) or (exists j in [i + 1, len(s)) . j >= i + 1 and j < len(s) and (s[j] < 'a' or s[j] > 'z')))
{
  var found: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) - 1
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s) - 1
    invariant found ==> exists k in [0, i) . k < len(s) - 1 and s[k] == '_' and (forall j in [0, k) . j >= 0 and j < k ==> s[j] >= 'a' and s[j] <= 'z') and (forall j in [k + 1, len(s)) . j >= k + 1 and j < len(s) ==> s[j] >= 'a' and s[j] <= 'z')
    invariant not found ==> (forall k in [0, i) . k < len(s) - 1 ==> s[k] != '_' or (exists j in [0, k) . j >= 0 and j < k and (s[j] < 'a' or s[j] > 'z')) or (exists j in [k + 1, len(s)) . j >= k + 1 and j < len(s) and (s[j] < 'a' or s[j] > 'z')))
    decreases len(s) - 1 - i
  {
    if not found and s[i] == '_' {
      var all_lower: bool := true;
      var j: int := 0;
      while j < len(s)
        invariant 0 <= j
        invariant j <= len(s)
        invariant all_lower ==> (forall k in [0, j) . k >= 0 and k < j and k != i ==> s[k] >= 'a' and s[k] <= 'z')
        invariant not all_lower ==> (exists k in [0, j) . k >= 0 and k < j and k != i and (s[k] < 'a' or s[k] > 'z'))
        decreases len(s) - j
      {
        if all_lower and j != i and (s[j] < 'a' or s[j] > 'z') {
          all_lower := false;
        } else {
        }
        j := j + 1;
      }
      if all_lower {
        found := true;
      } else {
      }
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if found {
    r := "Found a match!";
  } else {
    r := "Not matched!";
  }
}

task areEquivalent(a: int, b: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires a > 0
  requires b > 0
  ensures r == (sum_divs(a) == sum_divs(b))
spec fun sum_divs(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 0 then 0 else 1 + sum_divs_rest(n, 2)
spec fun sum_divs_rest(n: int, d: int): int
  decreases n - d
= if d * d > n then 0 else if n - d * (n / d) == 0 then d + (n / d) + sum_divs_rest(n, d + 1) else sum_divs_rest(n, d + 1)
{
  var sa: int := 0;
  var i: int := 1;
  while i <= a
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= a
    invariant sa == sum_divs_upto(a, i - 1)
    decreases a - i
  {
    if a - i * (a / i) == 0 {
      sa := sa + i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  var sb: int := 0;
  var j: int := 1;
  while j <= b
    invariant 0 <= j
    invariant j <= b
    invariant sb == sum_divs_upto(b, j - 1)
    decreases b - j
  {
    if b - j * (b / j) == 0 {
      sb := sb + j;
    } else {
    }
    j := j + 1;
  }
  r := (sa == sb);
}

task get_pell(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == pell(n)
  decreases n
spec fun pell(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 0 else if n == 1 then 1 else 2 * pell(n - 1) + pell(n - 2)
{
  if n == 0 {
    r := 0;
  } else {
    if n == 1 {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      var a: int := get_pell(n - 1);
      var b: int := get_pell(n - 2);
      r := 2 * a + b;
    }
  }
}

  }

task is_valid_parenthese(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == is_valid_parenthese_spec(s)
spec fun is_valid_parenthese_spec(s: seq): bool
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) == 0 then true
  else if len(s) == 1 then false
  else if s[0] == '(' and s[len(s) - 1] == ')' then is_valid_parenthese_spec(s[1..len(s) - 1])
  else if s[0] == '{' and s[len(s) - 1] == '}' then is_valid_parenthese_spec(s[1..len(s) - 1])
  else if s[0] == '[' and s[len(s) - 1] == ']' then is_valid_parenthese_spec(s[1..len(s) - 1])
  else false
{
  var stack: seq := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  var valid: bool := true;
  while i < len(s) and valid
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant valid ==> (forall k in [0, len(stack)) . (stack[k] == '(' or stack[k] == '{' or stack[k] == '['))
    invariant valid ==> (forall k in [0, i) . (s[k] == '(' or s[k] == '{' or s[k] == '[' or s[k] == ')' or s[k] == '}' or s[k] == ']'))
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == '(' or s[i] == '{' or s[i] == '[' {
      stack := stack + [s[i]];
    } else {
      if len(stack) == 0 {
        valid := false;
      } else {
        var top: int := stack[len(stack) - 1];
        if s[i] == ')' and top == '(' {
          stack := stack[0..len(stack) - 1];
        } else {
          if s[i] == '}' and top == '{' {
            stack := stack[0..len(stack) - 1];
          } else {
            if s[i] == ']' and top == '[' {
              stack := stack[0..len(stack) - 1];
            } else {
              valid := false;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if valid and len(stack) == 0 {
    r := true;
  } else {
    r := false;
  }
}

    invariant r == "Matched!" ==> exists j in [0, i) . j < len(lits) and s.find(lits[j]) != -1
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(lits)
    decreases len(lits) - i
  {
    if s.find(lits[i]) != -1 {
      r := "Matched!";
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }

task common_prefix(words: seq, n: int) returns (r: seq)
  requires n >= 0
  requires n <= len(words)
  requires forall i in [0, n) . i < len(words)
  ensures len(r) <= n
  ensures forall i in [0, n) . i < len(words) ==> r == words[i][0..len(r)]
  ensures len(r) == n or (forall i in [0, n) . i < len(words) ==> len(r) >= len(words[i]) or words[i][len(r)] != r[0])
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . j < len(words) ==> r == words[j][0..len(r)]
    invariant len(r) == i or (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(words) ==> len(r) >= len(words[j]) or words[j][len(r)] != r[0])
    decreases n - i
  {
    if i == 0 {
      r := words[0];
    } else {
      var j: int := 0;
      while j < len(r) and j < len(words[i]) and r[j] == words[i][j]
        invariant 0 <= j
        invariant j <= len(r)
        invariant j <= len(words[i])
        invariant forall k in [0, j) . k < len(r) and k < len(words[i]) ==> r[k] == words[i][k]
        decreases len(r) - j
      {
        j := j + 1;
      }
      r := r[0..j];
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task count_pairs(arr: seq, k: int, n: int) returns (r: int)
  ensures r >= 0
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(arr)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(arr)
    decreases len(arr) - i
  {
    var j: int := i + 1;
    while j < len(arr)
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= len(arr)
      decreases len(arr) - j
    {
      var diff: int := arr[i] - arr[j];
      if diff < 0 {
        diff := -diff;
      }
      if diff == k {
        r := r + 1;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task longest_common_subsequence(s: seq, t: seq, m: int, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires m >= 0
  requires n >= 0
  requires m <= len(s)
  requires n <= len(t)
  ensures r == lcs(s, t, m, n)
  decreases m + n
spec fun lcs(s: seq, t: seq, m: int, n: int): int
  decreases m + n
= if m == 0 or n == 0 then 0 else if s[m - 1] == t[n - 1] then 1 + lcs(s, t, m - 1, n - 1) else if lcs(s, t, m - 1, n) >= lcs(s, t, m, n - 1) then lcs(s, t, m - 1, n) else lcs(s, t, m, n - 1)
{
  if m == 0 or n == 0 {
    r := 0;
  } else {
    if s[m - 1] == t[n - 1] {
      r := 1 + longest_common_subsequence(s, t, m - 1, n - 1);
    } else {
      var a: int := longest_common_subsequence(s, t, m - 1, n);
      var b: int := longest_common_subsequence(s, t, m, n - 1);
      if a >= b {
        r := a;
      } else {
        r := b;
      }
    }
  }
}

task prod_Square(n: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == (exists a in [2, n+1) . exists b in [2, n+1) . a * a * b * b == n)
{
  r := false;
  var m: int := 0;
  var found: bool := false;
  while m * m <= n and not found
    invariant m >= 0
    invariant not found ==> (forall k in [0, m) . k * k != n)
    decreases n - m * m
  {
    if m * m == n {
      found := true;
    } else {
    }
    m := m + 1;
  }
  if found and m * m == n {
    var is_prime: bool := true;
    var d: int := 2;
    while d * d <= m and is_prime
      invariant d >= 2
      invariant is_prime ==> (forall k in [2, d) . k * k <= m ==> m % k != 0)
      decreases m - d * d
    {
      if m % d == 0 {
        is_prime := false;
      } else {
      }
      d := d + 1;
    }
    if m >= 4 and not is_prime {
      r := true;
    } else {
      r := false;
    }
  } else {
    r := false;
  }
}

task first_Missing_Positive(s: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r >= 1
  ensures (forall i in [0, n) . s[i] != r)
  ensures (forall i in [1, r) . exists j in [0, n) . s[j] == i)
{
  r := 1;
  var found: bool := true;
  while found
    invariant r >= 1
    invariant (forall i in [1, r) . exists j in [0, n) . s[j] == i)
    invariant found ==> (forall i in [0, n) . s[i] != r)
    decreases n
    {
      found := false;
      var j: int := 0;
      while j < n and not found
        invariant j >= 0
        invariant j <= n
        invariant (forall i in [1, r) . exists k in [0, n) . s[k] == i)
        invariant (forall k in [0, j) . s[k] != r)
        invariant found ==> s[j] == r
        decreases n - j
      {
        if s[j] == r {
          found := true;
        } else {
        }
        j := j + 1;
      }
      if found {
        r := r + 1;
      } else {
      }
    }
}

task check_String(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] >= 'a' and s[i] <= 'z' or s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z' and exists j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] >= '0' and s[j] <= '9')
{
  var has_letter: bool := false;
  var has_digit: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i <= len(s)
    invariant has_letter ==> exists k in [0, i) . s[k] >= 'a' and s[k] <= 'z' or s[k] >= 'A' and s[k] <= 'Z'
    invariant has_digit ==> exists k in [0, i) . s[k] >= '0' and s[k] <= '9'
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] >= 'a' and s[i] <= 'z' or s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z' {
      has_letter := true;
    } else {
    }
    if s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9' {
      has_digit := true;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := has_letter and has_digit;
}

task octal_To_Decimal(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == 0 or (forall k in [0, len(tostr(n))) . tostr(n)[k] >= '0' and tostr(n)[k] <= '7') ==> r == 0
  ensures (forall k in [0, len(tostr(n))) . tostr(n)[k] >= '0' and tostr(n)[k] <= '7') ==> r == 0
  ensures (forall k in [0, len(tostr(n))) . tostr(n)[k] >= '0' and tostr(n)[k] <= '7') ==> r == 0
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  var temp: int := n;
  while temp > 0
    invariant temp >= 0
    invariant i >= 0
    decreases temp
  {
    var digit: int := 0;
    var t2: int := temp;
    var j: int := 0;
    while t2 >= 10
      invariant t2 >= 0
      invariant j >= 0
      decreases t2
    {
      t2 := t2 - 10;
      j := j + 1;
    }
    digit := t2;
    r := r + digit * (8 * i);
    var t3: int := temp;
    var k: int := 0;
    while t3 >= 8
      invariant t3 >= 0
      invariant k >= 0
      decreases t3
    {
      t3 := t3 - 8;
      k := k + 1;
    }
    temp := t3;
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

  ensures r == -1 ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] != x)

    invariant r != -1 ==> r >= 0 and r < len(s) and s[r] == x and (forall j in [0, r) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] != x)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == x {
      r := i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task highest_Power_of_2(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r >= 1
  ensures r <= n
  ensures r == 1 or r % 2 == 0
  ensures 2 * r > n
  ensures (forall k in [1, r) . k % 2 == 0 ==> 2 * k <= n)
{
  r := 1;
  while 2 * r <= n
    invariant r >= 1
    invariant r <= n
    invariant r == 1 or r % 2 == 0
    invariant 2 * r > n or 2 * r <= n
    invariant (forall k in [1, r) . k % 2 == 0 ==> 2 * k <= n)
    decreases n - r
  {
    r := 2 * r;
  }
}

task is_woodall(x: int) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (exists n in [1, 1000) . n * 2^n - 1 == x)
{
  r := false;
  var n: int := 1;
  var p2: int := 2;
  while n * p2 - 1 <= x
    invariant n >= 1
    invariant p2 == 2^n
    invariant (forall k in [1, n) . k * 2^k - 1 != x) ==> r == false
    decreases x - (n * p2 - 1)
  {
    if n * p2 - 1 == x {
      r := true;
    } else {
    }
    n := n + 1;
    p2 := p2 * 2;
  }
}

task position_max(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  requires len(s) > 0
  ensures len(r) >= 1
  ensures forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] >= 0 and r[k] < len(s)
  ensures forall k in [0, len(r)) . s[r[k]] == s[r[0]]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == s[r[0]] ==> exists k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == i
  ensures forall k in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [0, k) . r[j] < r[k]
{
  var max_val: int := s[0];
  var i: int := 1;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant max_val == s[0] or (exists j in [0, i) . j < len(s) and s[j] == max_val)
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] <= max_val
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] > max_val {
      max_val := s[i];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := [];
  var j: int := 0;
  while j < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= j
    invariant j <= len(s)
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] >= 0 and r[k] < len(s) and s[r[k]] == max_val
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . forall l in [0, k) . r[l] < r[k]
    invariant forall i in [0, j) . i < len(s) and s[i] == max_val ==> exists k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == i
    decreases len(s) - j
  {
    if s[j] == max_val {
      r := r + [j];
    } else {
    }
    j := j + 1;
  }
}

task hamming_Distance(a: int, b: int) returns (r: int)
  requires a >= 0
  requires b >= 0
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r == hamming(a, b)
  decreases 0
spec fun hamming(a: int, b: int): int
  decreases a + b
= if a == 0 and b == 0 then 0 else if a == 0 then 1 + hamming(0, b - 1) else if b == 0 then 1 + hamming(a - 1, 0) else if (a % 2 == 0 and b % 2 == 0) or (a % 2 == 1 and b % 2 == 1) then hamming(a - 1, b - 1) else 1 + hamming(a - 1, b - 1)
{
  r := 0;
  var x: int := a;
  var y: int := b;
  while x != 0 or y != 0
    invariant r >= 0
    invariant r == hamming(a, b) - hamming(x, y)
    invariant x >= 0
    invariant y >= 0
    decreases x + y
  {
    var bx: int := 0;
    var by: int := 0;
    if x > 0 {
      bx := 1;
    } else {
    }
    if y > 0 {
      by := 1;
    } else {
    }
    if bx != by {
      r := r + 1;
    } else {
    }
    if x > 0 {
      x := x - 1;
    } else {
    }
    if y > 0 {
      y := y - 1;
    } else {
    }
  }
}

task find_longest_repeating_subseq(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r == 3 or r == 2 or r == 1 or r == 0
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant r >= 0
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    var j: int := i + 1;
    while j < len(s)
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= len(s)
      invariant r >= 0
      decreases len(s) - j
    {
      var k: int := 0;
      while k < len(s) - j
        invariant 0 <= k
        invariant k <= len(s) - j
        invariant r >= 0
        decreases len(s) - j - k
      {
        if s[i + k] == s[j + k] {
          if k + 1 > r {
            r := k + 1;
          } else {
          }
        } else {
        }
        k := k + 1;
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task multiples_of_num(m: int, n: int) returns (r: seq)
  requires m >= 0
  requires n >= 0
  ensures len(r) == m
  ensures forall i in [0, m) . r[i] == (i + 1) * n
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < m
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= m
    invariant len(r) == i
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . r[j] == (j + 1) * n
    decreases m - i
  {
    r := r + [(i + 1) * n];
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task is_allowed_specific_char(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (forall i in [0, len(s)) . (s[i] >= 'a' and s[i] <= 'z') or (s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z') or (s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9'))
{
  r := (forall i in [0, len(s)) . (s[i] >= 'a' and s[i] <= 'z') or (s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z') or (s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9'));
}

task count_Num(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == count_Num_spec(n)
  decreases n
spec fun count_Num_spec(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 0 else if (n and 1) == 1 and (n and 2) == 2 then 1 + count_Num_spec(n - 1) else count_Num_spec(n - 1)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i <= n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant r == count_Num_spec(i)
    decreases n - i
  {
    if (i and 1) == 1 and (i and 2) == 2 {
      r := r + 1;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task fourth_Power_Sum(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == (n * (n + 1) * (2 * n + 1) * (3 * n * n + 3 * n - 1)) / 30
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 1;
  while i <= n
    invariant i >= 1
    invariant i <= n + 1
    invariant r == (i - 1) * i * (2 * i - 1) * (3 * (i - 1) * (i - 1) + 3 * (i - 1) - 1) / 30
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    r := r + i * i * i * i;
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task first_Repeated_Char(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == 0 ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] != 0)
  ensures r != 0 ==> (exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == r and (exists j in [0, i) . s[j] == r))
  ensures r != 0 ==> (forall k in [0, len(s)) . (exists i in [0, k) . s[i] == s[k] and s[k] != 0) ==> s[k] == r)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and r == 0
    invariant r == 0 ==> (forall k in [0, i) . (forall j in [0, k) . s[j] != s[k] or s[k] == 0))
    invariant r != 0 ==> (exists i2 in [0, len(s)) . s[i2] == r and (exists j2 in [0, i2) . s[j2] == r))
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] != 0 {
      var j: int := 0;
      var found: bool := false;
      while j < i and not found
        invariant not found ==> (forall k in [0, j) . s[k] != s[i])
        invariant found ==> (exists k in [0, j) . s[k] == s[i])
        invariant j >= 0 and j <= i
        decreases i - j
      {
        if s[j] == s[i] {
          found := true;
        } else {
        }
        j := j + 1;
      }
      if found {
        r := s[i];
      } else {
      }
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task min_Operations(a: int, b: int) returns (r: int)
  requires a >= 0
  requires b >= 0
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r == 1 and (a * 2 == b or b * 2 == a) or
          r == 3 and (a == 1 and b == 4 or a == 4 and b == 1) or
          r == 4 and (a == 4 and b == 10 or a == 10 and b == 4) or
          r == 0 and a == b
{
  if a == b {
    r := 0;
  } else {
    if a * 2 == b or b * 2 == a {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      if a == 1 and b == 4 or a == 4 and b == 1 {
        r := 3;
      } else {
        if a == 4 and b == 10 or a == 10 and b == 4 {
          r := 4;
        } else {
          r := 0;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}
