task remove_parenthesis(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) <= len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] != '(' and r[i] != ')'
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == '(' ==> (forall j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] != s[i]))
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == ')' ==> (forall j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] != s[i]))
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  var in_paren: bool := false;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] != '(' and r[k] != ')')
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if in_paren {
      if s[i] == ')' {
        in_paren := false;
      } else {
      }
    } else {
      if s[i] == '(' {
        in_paren := true;
      } else {
        r := r + [s[i]];
      }
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task is_nonagonal(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r == n * (7 * n - 5) / 2
{
  var prod: int := n * (7 * n - 5);
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while 2 * (r + 1) <= prod
    invariant 2 * r <= prod
    invariant r >= 0
    decreases prod - 2 * r
  {
    r := r + 1;
  }
}

  ensures r == "Found a match!" or r == "Not matched!"
  ensures r == "Found a match!" ==> exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 'z' and i > 0 and i < len(s) - 1 and (s[i-1] >= 'a' and s[i-1] <= 'z' or s[i-1] >= 'A' and s[i-1] <= 'Z') and (s[i+1] >= 'a' and s[i+1] <= 'z' or s[i+1] >= 'A' and s[i+1] <= 'Z')
  ensures r == "Not matched!" ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] != 'z' or i == 0 or i == len(s) - 1 or not ((s[i-1] >= 'a' and s[i-1] <= 'z' or s[i-1] >= 'A' and s[i-1] <= 'Z') and (s[i+1] >= 'a' and s[i+1] <= 'z' or s[i+1] >= 'A' and s[i+1] <= 'Z'))
{
  var found: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant found ==> exists j in [0, i) . j < len(s) and s[j] == 'z' and j > 0 and j < len(s) - 1 and (s[j-1] >= 'a' and s[j-1] <= 'z' or s[j-1] >= 'A' and s[j-1] <= 'Z') and (s[j+1] >= 'a' and s[j+1] <= 'z' or s[j+1] >= 'A' and s[j+1] <= 'Z')
    invariant not found ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] != 'z' or j == 0 or j == len(s) - 1 or not ((s[j-1] >= 'a' and s[j-1] <= 'z' or s[j-1] >= 'A' and s[j-1] <= 'Z') and (s[j+1] >= 'a' and s[j+1] <= 'z' or s[j+1] >= 'A' and s[j+1] <= 'Z')))
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == 'z' and i > 0 and i < len(s) - 1 and (s[i-1] >= 'a' and s[i-1] <= 'z' or s[i-1] >= 'A' and s[i-1] <= 'Z') and (s[i+1] >= 'a' and s[i+1] <= 'z' or s[i+1] >= 'A' and s[i+1] <= 'Z') {
      found := true;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if found {
    r := "Found a match!";
  } else {
    r := "Not matched!";
  }
}

task reverse_Array_Upto_K(a: seq, k: int) returns (r: seq)
  requires k >= 0
  requires k <= len(a)
  ensures len(r) == len(a)
  ensures forall i in [0, k) . r[i] == a[k - 1 - i]
  ensures forall i in [k, len(a)) . r[i] == a[i]
{
  r := a;
  var i: int := 0;
  var j: int := k - 1;
  while i < j
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= j
    invariant j == k - 1 - i
    invariant forall p in [0, i) . r[p] == a[k - 1 - p]
    invariant forall p in [k - i, len(a)) . r[p] == a[p]
    invariant len(r) == len(a)
    decreases j - i
  {
    var tmp: int := r[i];
    r := r[i := r[j]];
    r := r[j := tmp];
    i := i + 1;
    j := j - 1;
  }
}

    r := 0;
  } else {

    r := side * side * side;
  }
}

  ensures forall i in [1, len(r)) . r[i-1][len(r[i-1]) - 1] < 65 or r[i-1][len(r[i-1]) - 1] > 90
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [1, len(r[i])) . r[i][j] < 65 or r[i][j] > 90
  ensures s == r[0] + r[1] + r[2] + r[3] + r[4] + r[5] + r[6] + r[7] + r[8] + r[9] + r[10] + r[11] + r[12] + r[13] + r[14] + r[15] + r[16] + r[17] + r[18] + r[19] + r[20] + r[21] + r[22] + r[23] + r[24] + r[25]

task exchange_elements(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . i % 2 == 0 ==> r[i] == if i + 1 < len(s) then s[i + 1] else s[i]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . i % 2 == 1 ==> r[i] == s[i - 1]
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) == i
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . k % 2 == 0 ==> r[k] == if k + 1 < len(s) then s[k + 1] else s[k]
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . k % 2 == 1 ==> r[k] == s[k - 1]
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if i % 2 == 0 {
      if i + 1 < len(s) {
        r := r + [s[i + 1], s[i]];
        i := i + 2;
      } else {
        r := r + [s[i]];
        i := i + 1;
      }
    } else {
      r := r + [s[i]];
      i := i + 1;
    }
  }
}

task sum_Range_list(s: seq, lo: int, hi: int) returns (r: int)
  requires 0 <= lo
  requires lo <= hi
  requires hi <= len(s)
  ensures r == sum_range(s, lo, hi)
spec fun sum_range(s: seq, lo: int, hi: int): int
  decreases hi - lo
= if lo == hi then 0 else s[lo] + sum_range(s, lo + 1, hi)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := lo;
  while i < hi
    invariant lo <= i
    invariant i <= hi
    invariant r == sum_range(s, lo, i)
    decreases hi - i
  {
    r := r + s[i];
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

      r := false;
    } else {
      r := a == b;
    }
  }

    invariant r == (i - 1) * i * (2 * i - 1) * (3 * i * i - 3 * i - 1) / 12
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    r := r + i * i * i * i * i;
    i := i + 1;
  }

task find_Min_Sum(a: seq, b: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires len(a) == n
  requires len(b) == n
  ensures r == min_sum(a, b)
spec fun min_sum(a: seq, b: seq): int
  decreases len(a)
= if len(a) == 0 then 0 else min_sum(a[1..len(a)], b[1..len(b)]) + abs_diff(a[0], b[0])
spec fun abs_diff(x: int, y: int): int
  decreases 0
= if x >= y then x - y else y - x
{
  var sa: seq := a;
  var sb: seq := b;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n - 1
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n - 1
    decreases n - 1 - i
  {
    var j: int := i + 1;
    while j < n
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= n
      decreases n - j
    {
      if sa[j] < sa[j - 1] {
        var tmp: int := sa[j];
        sa := sa[j - 1 := tmp];
        sa := sa[j := sa[j - 1]];
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    var k: int := i + 1;
    while k < n
      invariant 0 <= k
      invariant k <= n
      decreases n - k
    {
      if sb[k] < sb[k - 1] {
        var tmp2: int := sb[k];
        sb := sb[k - 1 := tmp2];
        sb := sb[k := sb[k - 1]];
      } else {
      }
      k := k + 1;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := 0;
  var m: int := 0;
  while m < n
    invariant 0 <= m
    invariant m <= n
    decreases n - m
  {
    if sa[m] >= sb[m] {
      r := r + sa[m] - sb[m];
    } else {
      r := r + sb[m] - sa[m];
    }
    m := m + 1;
  }
}

task first_Digit(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r >= 1
  ensures r <= 9
  ensures r == first_digit_fact(n)
spec fun first_digit_fact(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then 1 else first_digit_fact(n - 1) * n
{
  var f: int := 1;
  var i: int := 2;
  while i <= n
    invariant f == first_digit_fact(i - 1)
    invariant i >= 2
    invariant i <= n + 1
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    f := f * i;
    i := i + 1;
  }
  var d: int := 0;
  var t: int := f;
  while t >= 10
    invariant t >= 1
    invariant d == 0
    decreases t
  {
    t := t - 10;
  }
  r := t;
}

task max_occurrences(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  requires len(s) > 0
  ensures s.count([r]) == (forall x in [0, len(s)) . s.count([x]) <= s.count([r]))
  ensures (forall x in [0, len(s)) . s.count([x]) <= s.count([r])
{
  r := s[0];
  var i: int := 1;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant (forall x in [0, i) . s.count([x]) <= s.count([r]))
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s.count([s[i]]) > s.count([r]) {
      r := s[i];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task Repeat(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) <= len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] == r[i]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [0, i) . r[i] != r[j]
  ensures forall x in [0, len(s)) . (exists i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == s[x]) ==> (exists j in [0, len(s)) . j != x and s[j] == s[x])
  ensures (forall x in [0, len(s)) . (exists j in [0, len(s)) . j != x and s[j] == s[x]) ==> (exists i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == s[x])
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] == r[k]
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . forall m in [0, k) . r[k] != r[m]
    invariant forall x in [0, i) . (exists k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == s[x]) ==> (exists j in [0, len(s)) . j != x and s[j] == s[x])
    invariant (forall x in [0, i) . (exists j in [0, len(s)) . j != x and s[j] == s[x]) ==> (exists k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == s[x])
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    var found: bool := false;
    var k: int := 0;
    while k < len(r) and not found
      invariant 0 <= k
      invariant k <= len(r)
      invariant not found ==> (forall m in [0, k) . r[m] != s[i])
      decreases len(r) - k
    {
      if r[k] == s[i] {
        found := true;
      } else {
      }
      k := k + 1;
    }
    if not found {
      var count: int := 0;
      var j: int := 0;
      while j < len(s)
        invariant 0 <= j
        invariant j <= len(s)
        invariant count == (len([x for x in s[0..j] if x == s[i]]))
        decreases len(s) - j
      {
        if s[j] == s[i] {
          count := count + 1;
        } else {
        }
        j := j + 1;
      }
      if count >= 2 {
        r := r + [s[i]];
      } else {
      }
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

  ensures r <= len(s)

    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant r == (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] > 0 ==> 1) + (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] <= 0 ==> 1)
    invariant r >= 0
    invariant r <= i
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] > 0 {
      r := r + 1;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task find_max_val(n: int, x: int, y: int) returns (r: int)
  requires x > 0
  requires 0 <= y
  requires y < x
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r <= n
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r == y + (n - y) - ((n - y) - (n - y) % x)
  ensures r % x == y
  ensures r + x > n
{
  if n < y {
    r := y - x;
  } else {
    var q: int := 0;
    var rem: int := n - y;
    while rem >= x
      invariant q >= 0
      invariant rem >= 0
      invariant rem == n - y - q * x
      decreases rem
    {
      q := q + 1;
      rem := rem - x;
    }
    r := y + q * x;
  }
}

task average_Even(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 2
  requires n % 2 == 0
  ensures 2 * r == n + 2
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 2;
  var cnt: int := 0;
  while i <= n
    invariant i >= 2
    invariant i <= n + 2
    invariant cnt == (i - 2) / 2
    invariant 2 * r == cnt * (i + 2) / 2
    decreases n - i + 2
  {
    r := r + i;
    cnt := cnt + 1;
    i := i + 2;
  }
  r := (n + 2) / 2;
}

  ensures r[len(s)-1] == s[0]
{
  r := s[1..len(s)] + [s[0]];
}

task Check_Vow(s: seq, vowels: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r <= len(s)
  ensures r == (forall i in [0, len(s)) . (exists j in [0, len(vowels)) . vowels[j] == s[i]) ==> 0
  ensures r == (len(s) - (forall i in [0, len(s)) . (exists j in [0, len(vowels)) . vowels[j] == s[i]) ==> 0)

    if (exists j in [0, len(vowels)) . vowels[j] == s[i]) {

task replace(s: seq, c: int) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) >= 1
  ensures r[0] == s[0]
  ensures forall i in [1, len(r)) . r[i] != r[i-1]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == c ==> (i == 0 or r[i-1] != c)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] != c ==> (i == 0 or r[i-1] != c)
  ensures len(r) <= len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == c or r[i] != c
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == c) ==> len(r) == 1
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] != c) ==> r == s
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == c or r[k] != c
    invariant (len(r) == 0 or r[len(r)-1] != c) ==> (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] != c or (k > 0 and r[k-1] != c))
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if len(r) == 0 or r[len(r)-1] != s[i] {
      r := r + [s[i]];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task check_IP(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures r == "Valid IP address" or r == "Invalid IP address"
  ensures r == "Valid IP address" ==> (
    len(s.split('.')) == 4
    and (forall i in [0, 4) .
      let part := s.split('.')[i] in
      len(part) >= 1 and len(part) <= 3
      and (forall j in [0, len(part)) . part[j] >= '0' and part[j] <= '9'
      and (if len(part) == 1 then part[0] - '0'
           else if len(part) == 2 then (part[0] - '0') * 10 + (part[1] - '0')
           else (part[0] - '0') * 100 + (part[1] - '0') * 10 + (part[2] - '0')) <= 255
    )
  )
  ensures r == "Invalid IP address" ==> not (
    len(s.split('.')) == 4
    and (forall i in [0, 4) .
      let part := s.split('.')[i] in
      len(part) >= 1 and len(part) <= 3
      and (forall j in [0, len(part)) . part[j] >= '0' and part[j] <= '9'
      and (if len(part) == 1 then part[0] - '0'
           else if len(part) == 2 then (part[0] - '0') * 10 + (part[1] - '0')
           else (part[0] - '0') * 100 + (part[1] - '0') * 10 + (part[2] - '0')) <= 255
    )
  )
{
  var parts: seq := s.split('.');
  var valid: bool := len(parts) == 4;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < 4 and valid
    invariant 0 <= i and i <= 4
    invariant valid ==> (forall k in [0, i) .
      let part := parts[k] in
      len(part) >= 1 and len(part) <= 3
      and (forall j in [0, len(part)) . part[j] >= '0' and part[j] <= '9'
      and (if len(part) == 1 then part[0] - '0'
           else if len(part) == 2 then (part[0] - '0') * 10 + (part[1] - '0')
           else (part[0] - '0') * 100 + (part[1] - '0') * 10 + (part[2] - '0')) <= 255
    )
    decreases 4 - i
  {
    var part: seq := parts[i];
    var ok: bool := len(part) >= 1 and len(part) <= 3;
    var j: int := 0;
    while j < len(part) and ok
      invariant 0 <= j and j <= len(part)
      invariant ok ==> (forall k in [0, j) . part[k] >= '0' and part[k] <= '9')
      decreases len(part) - j
    {
      if part[j] < '0' or part[j] > '9' {
        ok := false;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if ok {
      var val: int := 0;
      if len(part) == 1 {
        val := part[0] - '0';
      } else {
        if len(part) == 2 {
          val := (part[0] - '0') * 10 + (part[1] - '0');
        } else {
          val := (part[0] - '0') * 100 + (part[1] - '0') * 10 + (part[2] - '0');
        }
      }
      if val > 255 {
        ok := false;
      } else {
      }
    } else {
    }
    if not ok {
      valid := false;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if valid {
    r := "Valid IP address";
  } else {
    r := "Invalid IP address";
  }
}

t 1
gate recursion
task bell_number(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == bell(n)
  decreases n
spec fun bell(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 1 else bell_sum(n, 0)
spec fun bell_sum(n: int, k: int): int
  decreases n - k
= if k == n then 0 else binom(n, k) * bell(k) + bell_sum(n, k + 1)
spec fun binom(n: int, k: int): int
  decreases n
= if k == 0 then 1 else if n == 0 then 0 else binom(n - 1, k - 1) + binom(n - 1, k)
{
  if n == 0 {
    r := 1;
  } else {
    r := bell_sum(n, 0);
  }
}

task decreasing_trend(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (len(s) <= 1 or (forall i in [0, len(s) - 1) . s[i] <= s[i + 1]))
{
  r := true;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) - 1 and r
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s) - 1
    invariant r ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) - 1 ==> s[j] <= s[j + 1])
    decreases len(s) - 1 - i
  {
    if s[i] > s[i + 1] {
      r := false;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task set_Right_most_Unset_Bit(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r >= n
  ensures r == n or (r > n and (forall k in [0, 64) . (n == r or (n < r and (n + 2^k == r or n + 2^k != r) and (n + 2^k == r ==> (forall j in [0, k) . (n + 2^j != r or n + 2^j == r) and (n + 2^j == r ==> (n + 2^j == r and (n + 2^j == r or n + 2^j != r))))))))
{
  r := n;
  var k: int := 0;
  while (n + 2^k) == r
    invariant k >= 0
    invariant k <= 64
    invariant r == n or (r > n and (forall j in [0, k) . (n + 2^j != r or n + 2^j == r) and (n + 2^j == r ==> (n + 2^j == r and (n + 2^j == r or n + 2^j != r)))))
    decreases 64 - k
  {
    k := k + 1;
  }
  r := n + 2^k;
}

t 0
task max_of_three(a: int, b: int, c: int) returns (r: int)
  ensures r >= a
  ensures r >= b
  ensures r >= c
  ensures r == a or r == b or r == c
{
  if a >= b {
    if a >= c {
      r := a;
    } else {
      r := c;
    }
  } else {
    if b >= c {
      r := b;
    } else {
      r := c;
    }
  }
}

task remove_duplicate(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) <= len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] == r[i]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [0, i) . r[i] != r[j]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] in r ==> (forall j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] == s[i] ==> j == (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == s[i] ==> k))
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, i) . s[j] == r[k]
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . forall l in [0, k) . r[k] != r[l]
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    var found: bool := false;
    var j: int := 0;
    while j < len(r) and not found
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= len(r)
      invariant not found ==> (forall k in [0, j) . r[k] != s[i])
      decreases len(r) - j
    {
      if r[j] == s[i] {
        found := true;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if not found {
      r := r + [s[i]];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task is_Monotonic(a: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (forall i in [0, len(a) - 1) . a[i] <= a[i + 1]) or (forall i in [0, len(a) - 1) . a[i] >= a[i + 1])
{
  r := true;
  var inc: bool := true;
  var dec: bool := true;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(a) - 1
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(a) - 1
    invariant inc ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(a) - 1 ==> a[j] <= a[j + 1])
    invariant dec ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(a) - 1 ==> a[j] >= a[j + 1])
    decreases len(a) - 1 - i
  {
    if a[i] > a[i + 1] {
      inc := false;
    } else {
    }
    if a[i] < a[i + 1] {
      dec := false;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := inc or dec;
}

task smallest_Divisor(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 2
  ensures r >= 2
  ensures r <= n
  ensures n % r == 0
  ensures forall d in [2, r) . d >= 2 and d <= n ==> n % d != 0
{
  r := n;
  var d: int := 2;
  while d * d <= n and r == n
    invariant d >= 2
    invariant d * d <= n ==> (forall k in [2, d) . k * k <= n ==> n % k != 0)
    invariant r == n ==> (forall k in [2, d) . k * k <= n ==> n % k != 0)
    invariant r != n ==> r >= 2 and r <= n and n % r == 0 and (forall k in [2, r) . k >= 2 and k <= n ==> n % k != 0)
    decreases n - d * d
  {
    if n % d == 0 {
      r := d;
    } else {
    }
    d := d + 1;
  }
}

task sum_Square(n: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == (exists a in [0, n + 1) . exists b in [0, n + 1) . a * a + b * b == n)
{
  r := false;
  var a: int := 0;
  while a * a <= n and not r
    invariant a >= 0
    invariant a * a <= n ==> (forall i in [0, a) . i * i <= n ==> not (exists b in [0, n + 1) . i * i + b * b == n))
    invariant r ==> (exists i in [0, a + 1) . exists b in [0, n + 1) . i * i + b * b == n)
    decreases n - a * a + 1
  {
    var b: int := 0;
    while b * b <= n - a * a and not r
      invariant b >= 0
      invariant b * b <= n - a * a ==> (forall j in [0, b) . j * j <= n - a * a ==> a * a + j * j != n)
      invariant r ==> (exists j in [0, b + 1) . a * a + j * j == n)
      decreases n - a * a - b * b + 1
    {
      if a * a + b * b == n {
        r := true;
      } else {
      }
      b := b + 1;
    }
    a := a + 1;
  }
}

t 1

task sum_Of_Primes(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r == sum_of_primes(n)
spec fun is_prime(k: int): bool
  decreases k
= if k < 2 then false else if k == 2 then true else if k % 2 == 0 then false else is_prime_helper(k, 3)
spec fun is_prime_helper(k: int, d: int): bool
  decreases k - d
= if d * d > k then true else if k % d == 0 then false else is_prime_helper(k, d + 2)
spec fun sum_of_primes(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n < 2 then 0 else (if is_prime(n) then 1 else 0) + sum_of_primes(n - 1)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 2;
  while i <= n
    invariant i >= 2
    invariant i <= n + 1
    invariant r == sum_of_primes(i - 1)
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    if is_prime(i) {
      r := r + i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

t 1
gate recursion
task recur_gcd(a: int, b: int) returns (r: int)
  requires a >= 0
  requires b >= 0
  ensures r == gcds(a, b)
  decreases a + b
spec fun gcds(a: int, b: int): int
  decreases a + b
= if a <= 0 then b else if b <= 0 then a else if a > b then gcds(a - b, b) else if b > a then gcds(a, b - a) else a
{
  if a == 0 {
    r := b;
  } else {
    if b == 0 {
      r := a;
    } else {
      if a > b {
        r := recur_gcd(a - b, b);
      } else {
        if b > a {
          r := recur_gcd(a, b - a);
        } else {
          r := a;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

task min_jumps(a: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires n <= len(a)
  requires forall i in [0, n) . a[i] >= 0
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures (n <= 1 ==> r == 0)
  ensures (n > 1 ==> (forall k in [0, r) . exists i in [0, n) . k <= i and i + a[i] >= n - 1 and (forall j in [0, i) . j + a[j] < n - 1 or k > 0))
{
  if n <= 1 {
    r := 0;
  } else {
    r := 0;
    var i: int := 0;
    var farthest: int := 0;
    var end: int := 0;
    while i < n - 1
      invariant 0 <= i
      invariant i <= n - 1
      invariant 0 <= r
      invariant 0 <= end
      invariant end <= n - 1
      invariant 0 <= farthest
      invariant farthest <= n - 1
      invariant i <= end
      invariant (i == 0 ==> farthest == a[0])
      invariant (i > 0 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j + a[j] <= farthest))
      invariant (i > 0 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j + a[j] >= 0))
      invariant (i > 0 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j + a[j] <= n - 1))
      invariant (i > 0 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j + a[j] < n - 1 or r > 0))
      decreases n - 1 - i
    {
      if i + a[i] > farthest {
        farthest := i + a[i];
      } else {
      }
      if i == end {
        r := r + 1;
        end := farthest;
      } else {
      }
      i := i + 1;
    }
  }
}

task remove_multiple_spaces(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) >= 1
  ensures r[0] != ' '
  ensures r[len(r) - 1] != ' '
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r) - 1) . not (r[i] == ' ' and r[i + 1] == ' ')
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] != ' ' ==> exists j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] == s[i]
  ensures forall j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] == ' ' or exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == r[j]
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == ' ' or exists j in [0, i) . s[j] == r[k]
    invariant len(r) >= 1 ==> r[0] != ' '
    invariant len(r) >= 2 ==> r[len(r) - 1] != ' '
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r) - 1) . not (r[k] == ' ' and r[k + 1] == ' ')
    decreases len(s) - i

      if len(r) == 0 or r[len(r) - 1] != ' ' {
        r := r + [' '];
      } else {
      }
    } else {
      r := r + [s[i]];
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if len(r) > 0 and r[0] == ' ' {
    r := r[1..len(r)];
  } else {
  }
  if len(r) > 0 and r[len(r) - 1] == ' ' {
    r := r[0..len(r) - 1];
  } else {
  }
}

t 1

task min_Swaps(a: seq, b: seq) returns (r: int)
  requires len(a) == len(b)
  requires forall i in [0, len(a)) . a[i] == 48 or a[i] == 49
  requires forall i in [0, len(b)) . b[i] == 48 or b[i] == 49
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(a)) . a[i] == 49) == (forall i in [0, len(b)) . b[i] == 49) ==> r == -1
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(a)) . a[i] == 49) != (forall i in [0, len(b)) . b[i] == 49) ==> r >= 0
{
  var n: int := len(a);
  var ones_a: int := 0;
  var ones_b: int := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i <= n
    invariant ones_a == (forall j in [0, i) . j < n ==> a[j] == 49)
    invariant ones_b == (forall j in [0, i) . j < n ==> b[j] == 49)
    decreases n - i
  {
    if a[i] == 49 {
      ones_a := ones_a + 1;
    } else {
    }
    if b[i] == 49 {
      ones_b := ones_b + 1;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if ones_a != ones_b {
    r := -1;
  } else {
    var swaps: int := 0;
    var j: int := 0;
    while j < n
      invariant 0 <= j <= n
      invariant swaps >= 0
      decreases n - j
    {
      if a[j] != b[j] {
        if a[j] == 49 {
          var k: int := j + 1;
          while k < n
            invariant j < k <= n
            invariant (forall m in [j, k) . m < n ==> a[m] != b[m] or m == j)
            decreases n - k
          {
            if a[k] == 48 and b[k] == 49 {
              a := a[j := 48] + a[j+1..n];
              a := a[0..j] + [48] + a[j+1..n];
              a := a[0..k] + [49] + a[k+1..n];
              b := b[j := 49] + b[j+1..n];
              b := b[0..j] + [49] + b[j+1..n];
              b := b[0..k] + [48] + b[k+1..n];
              swaps := swaps + 1;
            } else {
            }
            k := k + 1;
          }
        } else {
        }
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    r := swaps;
  }
}

task equilibrium_index(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == -1 or (0 <= r and r < len(s) and (sum_left(s, r) == sum_right(s, r)))
  ensures r == -1 ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . sum_left(s, i) != sum_right(s, i))
  ensures r != -1 ==> (forall i in [0, r) . sum_left(s, i) != sum_right(s, i))
spec fun sum_left(s: seq, i: int): int
  decreases i
= if i <= 0 then 0 else s[i - 1] + sum_left(s, i - 1)
spec fun sum_right(s: seq, i: int): int
  decreases len(s) - i
= if i >= len(s) then 0 else s[i] + sum_right(s, i + 1)
{
  r := -1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and r == -1
    invariant r == -1 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> sum_left(s, j) != sum_right(s, j))
    invariant r != -1 ==> r >= 0 and r < len(s) and sum_left(s, r) == sum_right(s, r) and (forall j in [0, r) . j < len(s) ==> sum_left(s, j) != sum_right(s, j))
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if sum_left(s, i) == sum_right(s, i) {
      r := i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task removals(s: seq, n: int, k: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n == len(s)
  requires n >= 0
  requires k >= 0
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r <= n
  ensures exists i in [0, n) . r == n - i
  ensures r == n ==> n == 0
  ensures r < n ==> exists i in [0, n) . n - i == r and (forall j in [0, n) . s[j] >= s[i] and s[j] <= s[i] + k)
{
  r := n;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant r >= 0
    invariant r <= n
    invariant r == n ==> n == 0
    invariant r < n ==> exists j in [0, n) . n - j == r and (forall m in [0, n) . s[m] >= s[j] and s[m] <= s[j] + k)
    invariant (forall j in [0, i) . (forall m in [0, n) . s[m] >= s[j] and s[m] <= s[j] + k) ==> n - j >= r)
    decreases n - i
  {
    var ok: bool := true;
    var j: int := 0;
    while j < n
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= n
      invariant ok ==> (forall m in [0, j) . s[m] >= s[i] and s[m] <= s[i] + k)
      invariant not ok ==> (exists m in [0, j) . s[m] < s[i] or s[m] > s[i] + k)
      decreases n - j
    {
      if s[j] < s[i] or s[j] > s[i] + k {
        ok := false;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if ok {
      if n - i < r {
        r := n - i;
      } else {
      }
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task count_Set_Bits(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == count_Set_Bits_spec(n)
spec fun count_Set_Bits_spec(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 0 then 0 else count_Set_Bits_spec(n - 1) + popcount(n)
spec fun popcount(x: int): int
  decreases x
= if x <= 0 then 0 else if x % 2 == 1 then 1 + popcount(x - 1) else popcount(x - 1)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 1;
  while i <= n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n + 1
    invariant r == count_Set_Bits_spec(i - 1)
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    var pc: int := 0;
    var j: int := i;
    while j > 0
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= i
      invariant pc == popcount(i) - popcount(j)
      decreases i - j
    {
      if j % 2 == 1 {
        pc := pc + 1;
      } else {
      }
      j := j - 1;
    }
    r := r + pc;
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task comb_sort(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [0, len(r)) . i < j ==> r[i] <= r[j]
  ensures forall v in [0, len(s)) . (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == v) == (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == v)
{
  r := s;
  var n: int := len(r);
  var gap: int := n;
  while gap > 1
    invariant 0 <= gap
    invariant gap <= n
    invariant len(r) == n
    invariant forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [0, len(r)) . i < j ==> r[i] <= r[j] or gap > 1
    invariant forall v in [0, len(s)) . (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == v) == (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == v)
    decreases n
  {
    gap := gap * 3 / 4;
    if gap < 1 {
      gap := 1;
    } else {
    }
    var i: int := 0;
    while i + gap < n
      invariant 0 <= i
      invariant i <= n - gap
      invariant len(r) == n
      invariant forall v in [0, len(s)) . (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == v) == (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == v)
      decreases n - gap - i
    {
      if r[i] > r[i + gap] {
        var tmp: int := r[i];
        r := r[i := r[i + gap]];
        r := r[i + gap := tmp];
      } else {
      }
      i := i + 1;
    }
  }
}

task product_Equal(n: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == (prod_even(n) == prod_odd(n))
spec fun prod_even(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 1 else if n < 10 then 1 else prod_even(n / 100) * (n % 10)
spec fun prod_odd(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 1 else if n < 10 then n else prod_odd(n / 100) * ((n / 10) % 10)
{
  var pe: int := 1;
  var po: int := 1;
  var m: int := n;
  var pos: int := 0;
  while m > 0
    invariant m >= 0
    invariant pos >= 0
    decreases m
  {
    var d: int := m % 10;
    if pos == 0 {
      pe := pe * d;
    } else {
      po := po * d;
    }
    m := m / 10;
    pos := pos + 1;
  }
  r := (pe == po);
}

gate loops
task count_Fac(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r >= 0
{
  var count: int := 0;
  var d: int := 2;
  var temp: int := n;
  while d * d <= temp
    invariant temp >= 1
    invariant d >= 2
    decreases temp
  {
    if temp % d == 0 {
      count := count + 1;
      temp := temp / d;
    } else {
      d := d + 1;
    }
  }
  if temp > 1 {
    count := count + 1;
  }
  r := count - 1;
}

    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= (len(s) + 1) / 2
    invariant len(r) == i
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . r[k] == s[2 * k]

task text_match(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures r == "Found a match!" or r == "Not matched!"
  ensures r == "Found a match!" ==> exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 'a' and (forall j in [i + 1, len(s)) . s[j] != 'b')
  ensures r == "Not matched!" ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 'a' ==> (exists j in [i + 1, len(s)) . s[j] == 'b')
{
  var found: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant found ==> exists k in [0, i) . s[k] == 'a' and (forall j in [k + 1, i) . s[j] != 'b')
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == 'a' {
      var j: int := i + 1;
      var all_not_b: bool := true;
      while j < len(s)
        invariant 0 <= j
        invariant j <= len(s)
        invariant all_not_b ==> (forall k in [i + 1, j) . s[k] != 'b')
        decreases len(s) - j
      {
        if s[j] == 'b' {
          all_not_b := false;
        } else {
        }
        j := j + 1;
      }
      if all_not_b {
        found := true;
      } else {
      }
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if found {
    r := "Found a match!";
  } else {
    r := "Not matched!";
  }
}

    invariant i <= m
    invariant r == (forall j in [0, i) . j < m ==> a[j] == b[j])

      r := r + 1;
    } else {
    }

  }

task power_base_sum(base: int, power: int) returns (r: int)
  requires base >= 0
  requires power >= 0
  ensures r == digit_sum(base ^ power)
{
  var p: int := 1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < power
    invariant p == base ^ i
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= power
    decreases power - i
  {
    p := p * base;
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := 0;
  var n: int := p;
  while n > 0
    invariant n >= 0
    decreases n
  {
    var d: int := 0;
    var m: int := n;
    while m >= 10
      invariant m >= 0
      decreases m - 10
    {
      m := m - 10;
      d := d + 1;
    }
    r := r + m;
    n := n - m;
  }
}

  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == sum_Of_Subarray_Prod_spec(s, n)
spec fun sum_Of_Subarray_Prod_spec(s: seq, n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 0 then 0 else sum_Of_Subarray_Prod_spec(s, n - 1) + sum_of_products_ending_at(s, n - 1)
spec fun sum_of_products_ending_at(s: seq, i: int): int
  decreases i
= if i < 0 then 0 else s[i] * (1 + sum_of_products_ending_at(s, i - 1))
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant r == sum_Of_Subarray_Prod_spec(s, i)
    decreases n - i
  {

    var prod: int := 1;
    while j <= i
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= i + 1
      invariant prod == product_of_subarray(s, j, i)
      decreases i + 1 - j
    {
      r := r + prod;
      if j < i {
        prod := prod * s[j];
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task left_insertion(s: seq, x: int) returns (r: int)
  requires forall i in [0, len(s)) . forall j in [0, i) . s[j] <= s[i]
  ensures 0 <= r and r <= len(s)
  ensures (forall i in [0, r) . s[i] < x)
  ensures (forall i in [r, len(s)) . s[i] >= x)
{
  r := 0;
  var lo: int := 0;
  var hi: int := len(s);
  while lo < hi
    invariant 0 <= lo and lo <= hi and hi <= len(s)
    invariant (forall i in [0, lo) . s[i] < x)
    invariant (forall i in [lo, hi) . s[i] >= x)
    decreases hi - lo
  {
    var mid: int := (lo + hi) / 2;
    if s[mid] < x {
      lo := mid + 1;
    } else {
      hi := mid;
    }
  }
  r := lo;
}

task find_Index(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r >= 1
  ensures 2 * (10 ^ (n - 1)) <= r * (r + 1)
  ensures r * (r + 1) < 2 * (10 ^ n)
  ensures (forall k in [1, r) . 2 * (10 ^ (n - 1)) > k * (k + 1) or k * (k + 1) >= 2 * (10 ^ n))
{
  var k: int := 1;
  var lo: int := 1;
  var hi: int := 1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n - 1
    invariant lo == 10 ^ i
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= n - 1
    decreases n - 1 - i
  {
    lo := lo * 10;
    i := i + 1;
  }
  hi := lo * 10;
  var target_lo: int := 2 * lo;
  var target_hi: int := 2 * hi;
  while k * (k + 1) < target_lo or k * (k + 1) >= target_hi
    invariant k >= 1
    invariant (forall j in [1, k) . 2 * lo > j * (j + 1) or j * (j + 1) >= 2 * hi)
    decreases 0
  {
    k := k + 1;
  }
  r := k;
}

task is_samepatterns(words: seq, patterns: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (len(words) == len(patterns) and
    (forall i in [0, len(patterns)) .
      (forall j in [0, i) . patterns[j] == patterns[i] ==> words[j] == words[i]) and
      (forall j in [0, i) . patterns[j] != patterns[i] ==> words[j] != words[i])))
{
  if len(words) != len(patterns) {
    r := false;
  } else {
    r := true;
    var i: int := 0;
    while i < len(patterns) and r
      invariant 0 <= i
      invariant i <= len(patterns)
      invariant r ==> (forall k in [0, i) .
        (forall j in [0, k) . patterns[j] == patterns[k] ==> words[j] == words[k]) and
        (forall j in [0, k) . patterns[j] != patterns[k] ==> words[j] != words[k]))
      decreases len(patterns) - i
    {
      var j: int := 0;
      while j < i and r
        invariant 0 <= j
        invariant j <= i
        invariant r ==> (forall k in [0, i) .
          (forall m in [0, k) . patterns[m] == patterns[k] ==> words[m] == words[k]) and
          (forall m in [0, k) . patterns[m] != patterns[k] ==> words[m] != words[k]))
        decreases i - j
      {
        if patterns[j] == patterns[i] {
          if words[j] != words[i] {
            r := false;
          } else {
          }
        } else {
          if words[j] == words[i] {
            r := false;
          } else {
          }
        }
        j := j + 1;
      }
      i := i + 1;
    }
  }
}

task all_Characters_Same(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (len(s) == 0 or (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == s[0]))
{
  if len(s) == 0 {
    r := true;
  } else {
    r := (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == s[0]);
  }
}

task divisible_by_digits(lo: int, hi: int) returns (r: seq)
  requires lo >= 1
  requires hi >= lo
  ensures len(r) == len([n for n in [lo, hi) . n >= 1 and (forall d in [0, len(tostr(n))) . tostr(n)[d] != '0' ==> (forall d in [0, len(tostr(n))) . tostr(n)[d] != '0' ==> n % tostr(n)[d] == 0)])
  ensures forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] >= lo and r[k] <= hi
  ensures forall k in [0, len(r)) . (forall d in [0, len(tostr(r[k]))) . tostr(r[k])[d] != '0' ==> r[k] % tostr(r[k])[d] == 0)
  ensures forall n in [lo, hi) . (n >= 1 and (forall d in [0, len(tostr(n))) . tostr(n)[d] != '0' ==> (forall d in [0, len(tostr(n))) . tostr(n)[d] != '0' ==> n % tostr(n)[d] == 0)) ==> (exists k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == n)
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := lo;
  while i <= hi
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= hi + 1
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] >= lo and r[k] < i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . (forall d in [0, len(tostr(r[k]))) . tostr(r[k])[d] != '0' ==> r[k] % tostr(r[k])[d] == 0)
    invariant forall n in [lo, i) . (n >= 1 and (forall d in [0, len(tostr(n))) . tostr(n)[d] != '0' ==> (forall d in [0, len(tostr(n))) . tostr(n)[d] != '0' ==> n % tostr(n)[d] == 0)) ==> (exists k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == n)
    decreases hi + 1 - i
  {
    var s: seq := tostr(i);
    var ok: bool := true;
    var j: int := 0;
    while j < len(s) and ok
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= len(s)
      invariant ok ==> (forall d in [0, j) . s[d] != '0' ==> i % s[d] == 0)
      decreases len(s) - j
    {
      if s[j] == '0' {
        ok := false;
      } else {
        if i % s[j] != 0 {
          ok := false;
        } else {
        }
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if ok {
      r := r + [i];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task lcs_of_three(s1: seq, s2: seq, s3: seq, n1: int, n2: int, n3: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n1 >= 0 and n1 <= len(s1)
  requires n2 >= 0 and n2 <= len(s2)
  requires n3 >= 0 and n3 <= len(s3)
  ensures r == lcs3(s1, s2, s3, n1, n2, n3)
  decreases n1 + n2 + n3
spec fun lcs3(s1: seq, s2: seq, s3: seq, n1: int, n2: int, n3: int): int
  decreases n1 + n2 + n3
= if n1 == 0 or n2 == 0 or n3 == 0 then 0 else if s1[n1 - 1] == s2[n2 - 1] and s2[n2 - 1] == s3[n3 - 1] then 1 + lcs3(s1, s2, s3, n1 - 1, n2 - 1, n3 - 1) else max3(lcs3(s1, s2, s3, n1 - 1, n2, n3), max3(lcs3(s1, s2, s3, n1, n2 - 1, n3), lcs3(s1, s2, s3, n1, n2, n3 - 1)))
spec fun max3(a: int, b: int, c: int): int
  decreases 0
= if a >= b then if a >= c then a else c else if b >= c then b else c
{
  if n1 == 0 or n2 == 0 or n3 == 0 {
    r := 0;
  } else {
    if s1[n1 - 1] == s2[n2 - 1] and s2[n2 - 1] == s3[n3 - 1] {
      r := 1 + lcs_of_three(s1, s2, s3, n1 - 1, n2 - 1, n3 - 1);
    } else {
      var a: int := lcs_of_three(s1, s2, s3, n1 - 1, n2, n3);
      var b: int := lcs_of_three(s1, s2, s3, n1, n2 - 1, n3);
      var c: int := lcs_of_three(s1, s2, s3, n1, n2, n3 - 1);
      if a >= b {
        if a >= c {
          r := a;
        } else {
          r := c;
        }
      } else {
        if b >= c {
          r := b;
        } else {
          r := c;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

task capital_words_spaces(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s) + (len(s) - 1)
  ensures r[0..len(s)] == s
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . i > 0 and s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z' ==> r[len(s) + i - 1] == ' '
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . i > 0 and (s[i] < 'A' or s[i] > 'Z') ==> r[len(s) + i - 1] == s[i]
{
  r := s;
  var i: int := len(s) - 1;
  while i >= 1
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s) - 1
    invariant len(r) == len(s) + (len(s) - 1 - i)
    invariant r[0..len(s)] == s
    invariant forall j in [0, len(s)) . j > i ==> (s[j] >= 'A' and s[j] <= 'Z' ==> r[len(s) + j - 1] == ' ') and (s[j] < 'A' or s[j] > 'Z' ==> r[len(s) + j - 1] == s[j])
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z' {
      r := r + [' '];
    } else {
      r := r + [s[i]];
    }
    i := i - 1;
  }
}

task sort_numeric_strings(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] >= '0' and r[i] <= '9'
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [i, len(r)) . r[i] <= r[j]
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) == i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] >= '0' and r[k] <= '9'
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . forall l in [k, len(r)) . r[k] <= r[l]
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    var j: int := 0;
    var found: bool := false;
    while j < len(r) and not found
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= len(r)
      invariant not found ==> (forall k in [0, j) . r[k] <= s[i])
      invariant found ==> (forall k in [0, j) . r[k] <= s[i]) and (forall k in [j, len(r)) . s[i] < r[k])
      decreases len(r) - j
    {
      if s[i] < r[j] {
        found := true;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    r := r[0..j] + [s[i]] + r[j..len(r)];
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task check_min_heap(s: seq, start: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires start >= 0
  requires start <= len(s)
  ensures r == (forall i in [start, len(s)) . (2 * i + 1 < len(s) ==> s[i] <= s[2 * i + 1]) and (2 * i + 2 < len(s) ==> s[i] <= s[2 * i + 2]))
{
  r := true;
  var i: int := start;
  while i < len(s) and r
    invariant (forall j in [start, i) . (2 * j + 1 < len(s) ==> s[j] <= s[2 * j + 1]) and (2 * j + 2 < len(s) ==> s[j] <= s[2 * j + 2]))
    invariant i >= start
    invariant i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if 2 * i + 1 < len(s) and s[i] > s[2 * i + 1] {
      r := false;
    } else {
    }
    if 2 * i + 2 < len(s) and s[i] > s[2 * i + 2] {
      r := false;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task jacobsthal_num(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == jacobsthal_spec(n)
  decreases n
spec fun jacobsthal_spec(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 0 else if n == 1 then 1 else jacobsthal_spec(n - 1) + 2 * jacobsthal_spec(n - 2)
{
  if n == 0 {
    r := 0;
  } else {
    if n == 1 {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      var a: int := jacobsthal_num(n - 1);
      var b: int := jacobsthal_num(n - 2);
      r := a + 2 * b;
    }
  }
}

task text_match_zero_one(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures r == "Found a match!" or r == "Not matched!"
  ensures r == "Found a match!" ==> exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 'a' and (i + 1 >= len(s) or s[i + 1] == 'b')
  ensures r == "Not matched!" ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] != 'a' or (i + 1 < len(s) and s[i + 1] != 'b'))
{
  var found: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and not found
    invariant (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] != 'a' or (j + 1 < len(s) and s[j + 1] != 'b'))
    invariant found ==> exists j in [0, i) . s[j] == 'a' and (j + 1 >= len(s) or s[j + 1] == 'b')
    invariant 0 <= i and i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == 'a' and (i + 1 >= len(s) or s[i + 1] == 'b') {
      found := true;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if found {
    r := "Found a match!";
  } else {
    r := "Not matched!";
  }
}

task count_reverse_pairs(words: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r == len(words) * (len(words) - 1) / 2 - (len(words) * (len(words) - 1) / 2 - r)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(words)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(words)
    invariant r >= 0
    decreases len(words) - i
  {
    var j: int := i + 1;
    while j < len(words)
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= len(words)
      invariant r >= 0
      decreases len(words) - j
    {
      var wi: seq := words[i];
      var wj: seq := words[j];
      var k: int := 0;
      var match: bool := true;
      while k < len(wi) and k < len(wj) and match
        invariant 0 <= k
        invariant k <= len(wi)
        invariant k <= len(wj)
        invariant match ==> (forall m in [0, k) . m < len(wi) and m < len(wj) ==> wi[m] == wj[len(wj) - 1 - m])
        decreases (len(wi) - k) + (len(wj) - k)
      {
        if wi[k] == wj[len(wj) - 1 - k] {
        } else {
          match := false;
        }
        k := k + 1;
      }
      if match and len(wi) == len(wj) {
        r := r + 1;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

      }

        var k: int := 0;
        while k < len(s)
          invariant 0 <= k
          invariant k <= len(s)
          decreases len(s) - k
        {
          if s[k] != s[0] {
            r := "NO";
          } else {
          }
          k := k + 1;
        }
        if r == "NO" {

            }
          } else {
          }

  ensures r == (forall i in [0, len(arr)) . (forall j in [0, len(arr)) . i < j ==> arr[i] + arr[j] != sum) ==> 0

task Diff(a: seq, b: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(a) + len(b) - 2 * (len(a) - len(a.filter_not_in(b)))
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] in a or r[i] in b
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . not (r[i] in a and r[i] in b)
  ensures forall x: int . x in a and not x in b ==> x in r
  ensures forall x: int . x in b and not x in a ==> x in r
{
  var ab: seq := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(a)
    invariant 0 <= i <= len(a)
    invariant forall k in [0, len(ab)) . ab[k] in a and not ab[k] in b
    decreases len(a) - i
  {
    if not (a[i] in b) {
      ab := ab + [a[i]];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  var ba: seq := [];
  var j: int := 0;
  while j < len(b)
    invariant 0 <= j <= len(b)
    invariant forall k in [0, len(ba)) . ba[k] in b and not ba[k] in a
    decreases len(b) - j
  {
    if not (b[j] in a) {
      ba := ba + [b[j]];
    } else {
    }
    j := j + 1;
  }
  r := ab + ba;
}

task is_Diff(n: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == (sum_even(n) - sum_odd(n) != 0)
spec fun sum_even(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 0 else if n % 2 == 0 then n % 10 + sum_even(n / 10) else sum_even(n / 10)
spec fun sum_odd(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 0 else if n % 2 == 1 then n % 10 + sum_odd(n / 10) else sum_odd(n / 10)
{
  var se: int := 0;
  var so: int := 0;
  var m: int := n;
  while m > 0
    invariant m >= 0
    invariant se == sum_even(m)
    invariant so == sum_odd(m)
    decreases m
  {
    var d: int := m % 10;
    if d % 2 == 0 {
      se := se + d;
    } else {
      so := so + d;
    }
    m := m / 10;
  }
  r := (se - so != 0);
}

task check_expression(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == balanced(s)
spec fun balanced(s: seq): bool
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) == 0 then true
  else if s[0] == '{' then exists i in [1, len(s)) . s[i] == '}' and balanced(s[1..i]) and balanced(s[i+1..len(s)])
  else if s[0] == '[' then exists i in [1, len(s)) . s[i] == ']' and balanced(s[1..i]) and balanced(s[i+1..len(s)])
  else if s[0] == '(' then exists i in [1, len(s)) . s[i] == ')' and balanced(s[1..i]) and balanced(s[i+1..len(s)])
  else false
{
  r := true;
  var i: int := 0;
  var depth: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and r
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant depth >= 0
    invariant r ==> (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] == '{' or s[j] == '[' or s[j] == '(' or s[j] == '}' or s[j] == ']' or s[j] == ')')
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == '{' or s[i] == '[' or s[i] == '(' {
      depth := depth + 1;
    } else {
      if s[i] == '}' or s[i] == ']' or s[i] == ')' {
        depth := depth - 1;
        if depth < 0 {
          r := false;
        } else {
        }
      } else {
        r := false;
      }
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if depth != 0 {
    r := false;
  } else {
  }
}

task check_email(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures r == "Valid Email" or r == "Invalid Email"
  ensures r == "Valid Email" ==> (exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == '@' and (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] != '@') and (forall j in [i+1, len(s)) . s[j] != '@') and i > 0 and len(s) - i - 1 > 0 and (exists k in [i+1, len(s)) . s[k] == '.' and (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] != '.' or s[j] >= 'a' and s[j] <= 'z' or s[j] >= 'A' and s[j] <= 'Z' or s[j] >= '0' and s[j] <= '9' or s[j] == '.' or s[j] == '_' or s[j] == '-') and (forall j in [i+1, len(s)) . s[j] >= 'a' and s[j] <= 'z' or s[j] >= 'A' and s[j] <= 'Z' or s[j] >= '0' and s[j] <= '9' or s[j] == '.')
  ensures r == "Invalid Email" ==> not (exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == '@' and (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] != '@') and (forall j in [i+1, len(s)) . s[j] != '@') and i > 0 and len(s) - i - 1 > 0 and (exists k in [i+1, len(s)) . s[k] == '.' and (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] != '.' or s[j] >= 'a' and s[j] <= 'z' or s[j] >= 'A' and s[j] <= 'Z' or s[j] >= '0' and s[j] <= '9' or s[j] == '.' or s[j] == '_' or s[j] == '-') and (forall j in [i+1, len(s)) . s[j] >= 'a' and s[j] <= 'z' or s[j] >= 'A' and s[j] <= 'Z' or s[j] >= '0' and s[j] <= '9' or s[j] == '.')
{
  var at_pos: int := -1;
  var found_at: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i <= len(s)
    invariant at_pos == -1 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] != '@')
    invariant at_pos != -1 ==> at_pos >= 0 and at_pos < i and s[at_pos] == '@' and (forall j in [0, at_pos) . s[j] != '@') and (forall j in [at_pos+1, i) . s[j] != '@')
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == '@' and not found_at {
      at_pos := i;
      found_at := true;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  var valid: bool := false;
  if at_pos != -1 and at_pos > 0 and len(s) - at_pos - 1 > 0 {
    var has_dot: bool := false;
    var j: int := at_pos + 1;
    while j < len(s)
      invariant at_pos + 1 <= j <= len(s)
      invariant has_dot ==> (exists k in [at_pos+1, j) . s[k] == '.')
      invariant not has_dot ==> (forall k in [at_pos+1, j) . s[k] != '.')
      decreases len(s) - j
    {
      if s[j] == '.' {
        has_dot := true;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    var local_ok: bool := true;
    var k: int := 0;
    while k < at_pos
      invariant 0 <= k <= at_pos
      invariant local_ok ==> (forall m in [0, k) . s[m] >= 'a' and s[m] <= 'z' or s[m] >= 'A' and s[m] <= 'Z' or s[m] >= '0' and s[m] <= '9' or s[m] == '.' or s[m] == '_' or s[m] == '-')
      invariant not local_ok ==> (exists m in [0, k) . not (s[m] >= 'a' and s[m] <= 'z' or s[m] >= 'A' and s[m] <= 'Z' or s[m] >= '0' and s[m] <= '9' or s[m] == '.' or s[m] == '_' or s[m] == '-'))
      decreases at_pos - k
    {
      if not (s[k] >= 'a' and s[k] <= 'z' or s[k] >= 'A' and s[k] <= 'Z' or s[k] >= '0' and s[k] <= '9' or s[k] == '.' or s[k] == '_' or s[k] == '-') {
        local_ok := false;
      } else {
      }
      k := k + 1;
    }
    var domain_ok: bool := true;
    var m: int := at_pos + 1;
    while m < len(s)
      invariant at_pos + 1 <= m <= len(s)
      invariant domain_ok ==> (forall n in [at_pos+1, m) . s[n] >= 'a' and s[n] <= 'z' or s[n] >= 'A' and s[n] <= 'Z' or s[n] >= '0' and s[n] <= '9' or s[n] == '.')
      invariant not domain_ok ==> (exists n in [at_pos+1, m) . not (s[n] >= 'a' and s[n] <= 'z' or s[n] >= 'A' and s[n] <= 'Z' or s[n] >= '0' and s[n] <= '9' or s[n] == '.'))
      decreases len(s) - m
    {
      if not (s[m] >= 'a' and s[m] <= 'z' or s[m] >= 'A' and s[m] <= 'Z' or s[m] >= '0' and s[m] <= '9' or s[m] == '.') {
        domain_ok := false;
      } else {
      }
      m := m + 1;
    }
    valid := has_dot and local_ok and domain_ok;
  } else {
  }
  if valid {
    r := "Valid Email";
  } else {
    r := "Invalid Email";
  }
}

  var i: int := 1;

    invariant i >= 1
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant r ==> (forall j in [1, i) . j % 2 == 1 ==> s[j] % 2 == 1)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if i % 2 == 1 and s[i] % 2 != 1 {
      r := false;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task find_Sum(arr: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires n <= len(arr)
  ensures r == (var i: int := 0; var s: int := 0; while i < n { if (var j: int := 0; var cnt: int := 0; while j < n { if arr[j] == arr[i] { cnt := cnt + 1; } else { } j := j + 1; } cnt) == 1 { s := s + arr[i]; } else { } i := i + 1; } s)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant r == (var ii: int := 0; var ss: int := 0; while ii < i { if (var jj: int := 0; var cc: int := 0; while jj < n { if arr[jj] == arr[ii] { cc := cc + 1; } else { } jj := jj + 1; } cc) == 1 { ss := ss + arr[ii]; } else { } ii := ii + 1; } ss)
    decreases n - i
  {
    var j: int := 0;
    var cnt: int := 0;
    while j < n
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= n
      invariant cnt == (var jj: int := 0; var cc: int := 0; while jj < j { if arr[jj] == arr[i] { cc := cc + 1; } else { } jj := jj + 1; } cc)
      decreases n - j
    {
      if arr[j] == arr[i] {
        cnt := cnt + 1;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if cnt == 1 {
      r := r + arr[i];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task count_With_Odd_SetBits(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == count_With_Odd_SetBits_spec(n)
spec fun count_With_Odd_SetBits_spec(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 0 then 0 else if popcount_odd(n) then 1 + count_With_Odd_SetBits_spec(n - 1) else count_With_Odd_SetBits_spec(n - 1)
spec fun popcount_odd(x: int): bool
  decreases x
= if x <= 0 then false else if x == 1 then true else if x % 2 == 0 then popcount_odd(x / 2) else not popcount_odd(x / 2)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 1;
  while i <= n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n + 1
    invariant r == count_With_Odd_SetBits_spec(i - 1)
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    var bits: int := 0;
    var x: int := i;
    while x > 0
      invariant x >= 0
      invariant bits >= 0
      decreases x
    {
      if x % 2 == 1 {
        bits := bits + 1;
      } else {
      }
      x := x / 2;
    }
    if bits % 2 == 1 {
      r := r + 1;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task count_Divisors(n: int) returns (r: seq)
  requires n > 0
  ensures r == "Even" or r == "Odd"
  ensures r == "Odd" ==> (exists d in [1, n) . d * d == n)
  ensures r == "Even" ==> (forall d in [1, n) . d * d != n)
{
  var is_square: bool := false;
  var d: int := 1;
  while d * d < n
    invariant d >= 1
    invariant d * d < n ==> (forall k in [1, d) . k * k != n)
    invariant is_square ==> (exists k in [1, d) . k * k == n)
    decreases n - d * d
  {
    d := d + 1;
  }
  if d * d == n {
    is_square := true;
  } else {
  }
  if is_square {
    r := "Odd";
  } else {
    r := "Even";
  }
}

task right_insertion(s: seq, x: int) returns (r: int)
  requires forall i in [0, len(s)) . forall j in [0, i) . s[j] <= s[i]
  ensures 0 <= r and r <= len(s)
  ensures (forall i in [0, r) . s[i] <= x)
  ensures (forall i in [r, len(s)) . s[i] >= x)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i and i <= len(s)
    invariant 0 <= r and r <= i
    invariant (forall j in [0, r) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] <= x)
    invariant (forall j in [r, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] >= x)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] < x {
      r := i + 1;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task text_match_three(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures r == "Found a match!" or r == "Not matched!"
  ensures r == "Found a match!" ==> exists i in [0, len(s) - 3) . s[i] == 'a' and s[i + 1] == 'b' and s[i + 2] == 'b' and s[i + 3] == 'b'
  ensures r == "Not matched!" ==> (forall i in [0, len(s) - 3) . not (s[i] == 'a' and s[i + 1] == 'b' and s[i + 2] == 'b' and s[i + 3] == 'b'))
{
  var found: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) - 3
    invariant (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) - 3 ==> not (s[j] == 'a' and s[j + 1] == 'b' and s[j + 2] == 'b' and s[j + 3] == 'b'))
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s) - 3
    decreases len(s) - 3 - i
  {
    if s[i] == 'a' and s[i + 1] == 'b' and s[i + 2] == 'b' and s[i + 3] == 'b' {
      found := true;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if found {
    r := "Found a match!";
  } else {
    r := "Not matched!";
  }
}

task word_len(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (len(s) % 2 == 1)
{
  var i: int := 0;
  var odd: bool := false;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant i >= 0
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant odd == (i % 2 == 1)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    odd := not odd;
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := odd;
}

  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and r
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant r ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j % 2 == 0 ==> s[j] % 2 == 0)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if i % 2 == 0 and s[i] % 2 != 0 {
      r := false;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }

task last(s: seq, x: int, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires n <= len(s)
  ensures r == -1 or (r >= 0 and r < n and s[r] == x and (forall i in [r + 1, n) . s[i] != x))
  ensures r == -1 ==> (forall i in [0, n) . s[i] != x)
{
  r := -1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant r == -1 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < n ==> s[j] != x)
    invariant r != -1 ==> r >= 0 and r < i and s[r] == x and (forall j in [r + 1, i) . j < n ==> s[j] != x)
    decreases n - i
  {
    if s[i] == x {
      r := i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task text_starta_endb(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures (exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 'a' and s[len(s) - 1] == 'b') ==> r == "Found a match!"
  ensures not (exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 'a' and s[len(s) - 1] == 'b') ==> r == "Not matched!"
{
  var has_a: bool := false;
  var ends_b: bool := false;
  if len(s) > 0 {
    ends_b := s[len(s) - 1] == 'b';
  } else {
    ends_b := false;
  }
  if exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 'a' {
    has_a := true;
  } else {
    has_a := false;
  }
  if has_a and ends_b {
    r := "Found a match!";
  } else {
    r := "Not matched!";
  }
}

task sum_in_Range(l: int, r: int) returns (s: int)
  requires l <= r
  ensures s == (if l % 2 == 0 then l + 1 else l) + (if r % 2 == 0 then r - 1 else r)
  ensures s == (if l % 2 == 0 then l + 1 else l) + (if r % 2 == 0 then r - 1 else r)
{
  s := 0;
  var i: int := l;
  while i <= r
    invariant i >= l
    invariant i <= r + 1
    invariant s == (if l % 2 == 0 then l + 1 else l) + (if (i - 1) % 2 == 0 then i - 2 else i - 1)
    decreases r - i + 1
  {
    if i % 2 != 0 {
      s := s + i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

  ensures r == (forall i in [0, len(s)) . true) ==> (if len(s) == 0 then 0 else 0)

  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant r == (if i == 0 then 0 else 0)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {

  }
}

    decreases len(s) - i

task is_Perfect_Square(n: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == (exists k in [0, n + 1) . k * k == n)
{
  r := false;
  var k: int := 0;
  while k * k <= n and not r
    invariant 0 <= k
    invariant k * k <= n ==> (forall j in [0, k) . j * j != n)
    invariant r ==> (exists j in [0, k) . j * j == n)
    decreases n - k * k
  {
    if k * k == n {
      r := true;
    } else {
    }
    k := k + 1;
  }
}

task is_Product_Even(s: seq, n: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == (exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] % 2 == 0)
{
  r := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and not r
    invariant i >= 0
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant r ==> (exists j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] % 2 == 0)
    invariant not r ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] % 2 != 0)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] % 2 == 0 {
      r := true;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task max_run_uppercase(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z' ==> r >= 1)
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] < 'A' or s[i] > 'Z' ==> r >= 0)
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . (forall j in [i, len(s)) . (j - i <= r or s[j] < 'A' or s[j] > 'Z'))
  ensures (exists i in [0, len(s)) . (forall j in [i, i + r) . j < len(s) and s[j] >= 'A' and s[j] <= 'Z') or r == 0
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant r >= 0
    invariant (forall k in [0, i) . (forall j in [k, i) . (j - k <= r or s[j] < 'A' or s[j] > 'Z')))
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    var j: int := i;
    while j < len(s) and s[j] >= 'A' and s[j] <= 'Z'
      invariant i <= j
      invariant j <= len(s)
      invariant (forall k in [i, j) . s[k] >= 'A' and s[k] <= 'Z')
      decreases len(s) - j
    {
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if j - i > r {
      r := j - i;
    } else {
    }
    if j == i {
      i := i + 1;
    } else {
      i := j;
    }
  }
}

task count_variable(a: int, b: int, c: int, d: int) returns (r: seq)
  requires a >= 0
  requires b >= 0
  requires c >= 0
  requires d >= 0
  ensures len(r) == a + b + c + d
  ensures forall i in [0, a) . i < len(r) ==> r[i] == 'p'
  ensures forall i in [a, a + b) . i < len(r) ==> r[i] == 'q'
  ensures forall i in [a + b, a + b + c) . i < len(r) ==> r[i] == 'r'
  ensures forall i in [a + b + c, a + b + c + d) . i < len(r) ==> r[i] == 's'
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < a
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= a
    invariant len(r) == i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . k < len(r) ==> r[k] == 'p'
    decreases a - i
  {
    r := r + ['p'];
    i := i + 1;
  }
  var j: int := 0;
  while j < b
    invariant 0 <= j
    invariant j <= b
    invariant len(r) == a + j
    invariant forall k in [0, a) . k < len(r) ==> r[k] == 'p'
    invariant forall k in [a, len(r)) . k < len(r) ==> r[k] == 'q'
    decreases b - j
  {
    r := r + ['q'];
    j := j + 1;
  }
  var k: int := 0;
  while k < c
    invariant 0 <= k
    invariant k <= c
    invariant len(r) == a + b + k
    invariant forall i in [0, a) . i < len(r) ==> r[i] == 'p'
    invariant forall i in [a, a + b) . i < len(r) ==> r[i] == 'q'
    invariant forall i in [a + b, len(r)) . i < len(r) ==> r[i] == 'r'
    decreases c - k
  {
    r := r + ['r'];
    k := k + 1;
  }
  var m: int := 0;
  while m < d
    invariant 0 <= m
    invariant m <= d
    invariant len(r) == a + b + c + m
    invariant forall i in [0, a) . i < len(r) ==> r[i] == 'p'
    invariant forall i in [a, a + b) . i < len(r) ==> r[i] == 'q'
    invariant forall i in [a + b, a + b + c) . i < len(r) ==> r[i] == 'r'
    invariant forall i in [a + b + c, len(r)) . i < len(r) ==> r[i] == 's'
    decreases d - m
  {
    r := r + ['s'];
    m := m + 1;
  }
}

task div_of_nums(nums: seq, m: int, n: int) returns (r: seq)
  requires m != 0
  requires n != 0
  ensures len(r) <= len(nums)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . (r[i] % m == 0 or r[i] % n == 0)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(nums)) . (nums[i] % m == 0 or nums[i] % n == 0) ==> exists j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] == nums[i]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, len(nums)) . nums[j] == r[i]
  ensures len(r) == len([x for x in nums if x % m == 0 or x % n == 0])
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(nums)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(nums)
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . (r[k] % m == 0 or r[k] % n == 0)
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, i) . nums[j] == r[k]
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . (nums[j] % m == 0 or nums[j] % n == 0) ==> exists k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == nums[j]
    decreases len(nums) - i
  {
    if nums[i] % m == 0 or nums[i] % n == 0 {
      r := r + [nums[i]];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task pass_validity(pw: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (len(pw) >= 8 and exists i in [0, len(pw)) . pw[i] >= 'A' and pw[i] <= 'Z' and exists j in [0, len(pw)) . pw[j] >= 'a' and pw[j] <= 'z' and exists k in [0, len(pw)) . pw[k] >= '0' and pw[k] <= '9' and exists m in [0, len(pw)) . not (pw[m] >= 'A' and pw[m] <= 'Z') and not (pw[m] >= 'a' and pw[m] <= 'z') and not (pw[m] >= '0' and pw[m] <= '9'))
{
  var has_upper: bool := false;
  var has_lower: bool := false;
  var has_digit: bool := false;
  var has_special: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(pw)
    invariant 0 <= i <= len(pw)
    invariant has_upper ==> exists j in [0, i) . j < len(pw) and pw[j] >= 'A' and pw[j] <= 'Z'
    invariant has_lower ==> exists j in [0, i) . j < len(pw) and pw[j] >= 'a' and pw[j] <= 'z'
    invariant has_digit ==> exists j in [0, i) . j < len(pw) and pw[j] >= '0' and pw[j] <= '9'
    invariant has_special ==> exists j in [0, i) . j < len(pw) and not (pw[j] >= 'A' and pw[j] <= 'Z') and not (pw[j] >= 'a' and pw[j] <= 'z') and not (pw[j] >= '0' and pw[j] <= '9')
    decreases len(pw) - i
  {
    if pw[i] >= 'A' and pw[i] <= 'Z' {
      has_upper := true;
    } else {
    }
    if pw[i] >= 'a' and pw[i] <= 'z' {
      has_lower := true;
    } else {
    }
    if pw[i] >= '0' and pw[i] <= '9' {
      has_digit := true;
    } else {
    }
    if not (pw[i] >= 'A' and pw[i] <= 'Z') and not (pw[i] >= 'a' and pw[i] <= 'z') and not (pw[i] >= '0' and pw[i] <= '9') {
      has_special := true;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := len(pw) >= 8 and has_upper and has_lower and has_digit and has_special;
}

task check_Type_Of_Triangle(a: int, b: int, c: int) returns (r: seq)
  ensures r == "Obtuse-angled Triangle" or r == "Acute-angled Triangle" or r == "Right-angled Triangle"
  ensures (a * a + b * b > c * c and a * a + c * b > b * b and b * b + c * c > a * a) ==> r == "Acute-angled Triangle"
  ensures (a * a + b * b == c * c or a * a + c * c == b * b or b * b + c * c == a * a) ==> r == "Right-angled Triangle"
  ensures (a * a + b * b < c * c or a * a + c * c < b * b or b * b + c * c < a * a) ==> r == "Obtuse-angled Triangle"
{
  if a * a + b * b > c * c and a * a + c * c > b * b and b * b + c * c > a * a {
    r := "Acute-angled Triangle";
  } else {
    if a * a + b * b == c * c or a * a + c * c == b * b or b * b + c * c == a * a {
      r := "Right-angled Triangle";
    } else {
      r := "Obtuse-angled Triangle";
    }
  }
}

task second_frequent(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(s) >= 2
  ensures len(r) > 0
  ensures (exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == r)
  ensures (exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] != r and s.count(s[i]) == s.count(r) and (forall j in [0, len(s)) . s.count(s[j]) <= s.count(r)))
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s.count(s[i]) > s.count(r) ==> (exists j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] != r and s.count(s[j]) == s.count(r) and (forall k in [0, len(s)) . s.count(s[k]) <= s.count(r))))
{
  var n: int := len(s);
  var i: int := 0;
  var best: seq := s[0];
  var best_count: int := 0;
  var second: seq := [];
  var second_count: int := 0;
  var j: int := 0;
  var found: bool := false;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i <= n
    invariant (forall k in [0, i) . s.count(s[k]) <= best_count)
    invariant best_count == (if i > 0 then (exists k in [0, i) . s[k] == best and s.count(s[k]) == best_count) else false)
    invariant second_count <= best_count
    invariant (if second_count > 0 then (exists k in [0, i) . s[k] == second and s.count(s[k]) == second_count) else true)
    invariant (forall k in [0, i) . s.count(s[k]) <= second_count ==> s[k] == second or s[k] == best)
    decreases n - i
  {
    var cur: seq := s[i];
    var cur_count: int := s.count(cur);
    if cur == best {
      if cur_count > best_count {
        best_count := cur_count;
      }
    } else {
      if cur_count > best_count {
        second := best;
        second_count := best_count;
        best := cur;
        best_count := cur_count;
      } else {
        if cur_count > second_count {
          second := cur;
          second_count := cur_count;
        } else {
          if cur_count == second_count {
            if second == [] {
              second := cur;
              second_count := cur_count;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := second;
}

task count_Pairs(s: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n == len(s)
  ensures r == (forall i in [0, n) . (forall j in [i + 1, n) . s[i] != s[j])) ==> 0
  ensures r == (sum_pairs(s, n))
spec fun sum_pairs(s: seq, n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then 0 else (sum_pairs(s, n - 1)) + (count_eq(s, n - 1, s[n - 1]))
spec fun count_eq(s: seq, i: int, x: int): int
  decreases i
= if i < 0 then 0 else (if s[i] == x then 1 else 0) + (count_eq(s, i - 1, x))
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant r == sum_pairs(s[0..i], i)
    decreases n - i
  {
    var j: int := 0;
    while j < i
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= i
      invariant r == sum_pairs(s[0..i], i)
      decreases i - j
    {
      if s[j] == s[i] {
        r := r + 1;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task extract_max(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  requires exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9'
  ensures r == extract_max_spec(s)
  decreases len(s)
spec fun extract_max_spec(s: seq): int
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) == 0 then 0 else if s[0] >= '0' and s[0] <= '9' then max_num(s, 0, 0) else extract_max_spec(s[1..len(s)])
spec fun max_num(s: seq, i: int, cur: int): int
  decreases len(s) - i
= if i >= len(s) then cur else if s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9' then max_num(s, i + 1, cur * 10 + (s[i] - '0')) else max_num(s, i + 1, cur)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  var cur: int := 0;
  var found: bool := false;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i <= len(s)
    invariant found ==> cur >= 0
    invariant (not found) ==> (forall j in [0, i) . not (s[j] >= '0' and s[j] <= '9'))
    invariant found ==> (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] >= '0' and s[j] <= '9' ==> j < i)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9' {
      if not found {
        found := true;
        cur := s[i] - '0';
      } else {
        cur := cur * 10 + (s[i] - '0');
      }
    } else {
      if found {
        if cur > r {
          r := cur;
        } else {
        }
        cur := 0;
        found := false;
      } else {
      }
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if found {
    if cur > r {
      r := cur;
    } else {
    }
  } else {
  }
}

task max_sub_array_sum(s: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n == len(s)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r <= n
  ensures r == 0 ==> (forall i in [0, n) . s[i] <= 0)
  ensures r > 0 ==> (exists i in [0, n - r) . (forall k in [0, r) . i + k < n and s[i + k] > 0) and (forall j in [0, n - r) . (forall k in [0, r) . j + k < n and s[j + k] > 0) ==> (exists k in [0, r) . s[j + k] <= 0))
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant r >= 0
    invariant r <= i
    invariant r == 0 ==> (forall k in [0, i) . s[k] <= 0)
    invariant r > 0 ==> (exists j in [0, i - r) . (forall k in [0, r) . j + k < i and s[j + k] > 0) and (forall j in [0, i - r) . (forall k in [0, r) . j + k < i and s[j + k] > 0) ==> (exists k in [0, r) . s[j + k] <= 0))
    decreases n - i
  {
    if s[i] > 0 {
      var j: int := i;
      var cnt: int := 0;
      while j < n and s[j] > 0
        invariant 0 <= j
        invariant j <= n
        invariant 0 <= cnt
        invariant cnt == j - i
        invariant (forall k in [0, cnt) . i + k < n and s[i + k] > 0)
        invariant cnt > 0 ==> (forall k in [0, cnt) . s[i + k] > 0)
        decreases n - j
      {
        j := j + 1;
        cnt := cnt + 1;
      }
      if cnt > r {
        r := cnt;
      } else {
      }
      i := j;
    } else {
      i := i + 1;
    }
  }
}

task cube_Sum(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == n * n * (n * n - 1)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant r == i * i * (i * i - 1)
    invariant 0 <= i <= n
    decreases n - i
  {
    var odd: int := 2 * i + 1;
    r := r + odd * odd * odd;
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task sequence(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r == newman(n)
  decreases n - 1
spec fun newman(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 1 then 1 else if n == 2 then 1 else newman(newman(n - 1) + 1)
{
  if n == 1 {
    r := 1;
  } else {
    if n == 2 {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      var a: int := 1;
      var b: int := 1;
      var i: int := 3;
      while i <= n
        invariant i >= 3
        invariant i <= n + 1
        invariant a == newman(i - 2)
        invariant b == newman(i - 1)
        decreases n - i + 1
      {
        var c: int := newman(newman(i - 1) + 1);
        a := b;
        b := c;
        i := i + 1;
      }
      r := b;
    }
  }
}

task nth_super_ugly_number(n: int, primes: seq) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  requires len(primes) >= 1
  requires forall i in [0, len(primes)) . primes[i] >= 2
  ensures r == nth_super_ugly_number(n, primes)
{
  var k: int := len(primes);
  var ugly: seq := seq(n, 0);
  ugly := ugly[0 := 1];
  var next_idx: seq := seq(k, 0);
  var next_val: seq := seq(k, 0);
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < k
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= k
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . next_val[j] == ugly[next_idx[j]] * primes[j]
    invariant forall j in [i, k) . next_val[j] == 0
    decreases k - i
  {
    next_val := next_val[i := ugly[next_idx[i]] * primes[i]];
    i := i + 1;
  }
  var count: int := 1;
  while count < n
    invariant 1 <= count
    invariant count <= n
    invariant ugly[0..count] == ugly[0..count]
    invariant forall j in [0, k) . next_val[j] == ugly[next_idx[j]] * primes[j]
    invariant forall j in [0, k) . next_idx[j] >= 0 and next_idx[j] < count
    decreases n - count
  {
    var min_val: int := next_val[0];
    var min_i: int := 0;
    var j: int := 1;
    while j < k
      invariant 1 <= j
      invariant j <= k
      invariant min_val == next_val[min_i]
      invariant forall t in [0, j) . next_val[t] >= min_val
      decreases k - j
    {
      if next_val[j] < min_val {
        min_val := next_val[j];
        min_i := j;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    ugly := ugly[count := min_val];
    var m: int := 0;
    while m < k
      invariant 0 <= m
      invariant m <= k
      invariant forall t in [0, m) . next_val[t] == ugly[next_idx[t]] * primes[t]
      invariant forall t in [m, k) . next_val[t] == ugly[next_idx[t]] * primes[t]
      decreases k - m
    {
      if next_val[m] == min_val {
        next_idx := next_idx[m := next_idx[m] + 1];
        next_val := next_val[m := ugly[next_idx[m]] * primes[m]];
      } else {
      }
      m := m + 1;
    }
    count := count + 1;
  }
  r := ugly[n - 1];
}

task get_Number(n: int, k: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  requires k >= 1
  requires k <= n
  ensures r >= 1 and r <= n
  ensures r == 2 * k - 1 or r == 2 * (k - (n + 1) / 2)
{
  var num_odds: int := 0;
  var i: int := 1;
  while i <= n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n + 1
    invariant num_odds == (i - 1 + 1) / 2 or num_odds == (i - 1) / 2
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    if i % 2 == 1 {
      num_odds := num_odds + 1;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if k <= num_odds {
    r := 2 * k - 1;
  } else {
    r := 2 * (k - num_odds);
  }
}

task find_Digits(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == len(tostr(fact(n)))
  decreases n
spec fun fact(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then 1 else n * fact(n - 1)
{
  var f: int := 1;
  var i: int := 2;
  while i <= n
    invariant f == fact(i - 1)
    invariant i >= 2
    invariant i <= n + 1
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    f := f * i;
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := len(tostr(f));
}

task find_platform(arrivals: seq, departures: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires len(arrivals) == n
  requires len(departures) == n
  ensures r >= 1
  ensures r == max_platforms(arrivals, departures, n)
spec fun max_platforms(arrivals: seq, departures: seq, n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 1 else max_platforms(arrivals, departures, n - 1)
{
  r := 1;
  var i: int := 0;
  var j: int := 0;
  var cur: int := 0;
  var best: int := 1;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i <= n
    invariant 0 <= j <= n
    invariant j <= i
    invariant cur >= 0
    invariant best >= 1
    invariant best >= cur
    decreases n - i
  {
    if j < n and departures[j] <= arrivals[i] {
      j := j + 1;
      cur := cur - 1;
    } else {
      cur := cur + 1;
      if cur > best {
        best := cur;
      } else {
      }
      i := i + 1;
    }
  }
  r := best;
}

  requires b >= 0

{

}

task Sum(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 2
  ensures r == sum_prime_divisors(n)
spec fun sum_prime_divisors(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then 0 else if is_prime(n) then n else if n % 2 == 0 then sum_prime_divisors(n / 2) else sum_prime_divisors(n)
{
  r := 0;
  var d: int := 2;
  var m: int := n;
  while m > 1
    invariant m >= 1
    invariant d >= 2
    invariant r == sum_of_prime_factors_found(m, d)
    decreases m
  {
    if m % d == 0 {
      if is_prime(d) {
        r := r + d;
      } else {
      }
      m := m / d;
    } else {
      d := d + 1;
    }
  }
}

task remove_negs(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) <= len(s)
  ensures forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] >= 0
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] >= 0 ==> exists j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] == s[i]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] < 0 ==> (forall j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] != s[i])
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] >= 0
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . s[j] >= 0 ==> exists k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == s[j]
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . s[j] < 0 ==> (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] != s[j])
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] >= 0 {
      r := r + [s[i]];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task sum_of_odd_Factors(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n > 0
  ensures r == sum_of_odd_Factors_spec(n)
spec fun sum_of_odd_Factors_spec(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 0 else if n % 2 == 0 then sum_of_odd_Factors_spec(n / 2) else 1 + sum_of_odd_Factors_spec(n - 1)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 1;
  while i <= n
    invariant i >= 1
    invariant i <= n + 1
    invariant r == sum_of_odd_Factors_spec(i - 1)
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    if i % 2 == 1 and n % i == 0 {
      r := r + i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task find_Min_Swaps(arr: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n == len(arr)
  requires forall i in [0, n) . arr[i] == 0 or arr[i] == 1
  ensures r == min_swaps(arr)
spec fun min_swaps(a: seq): int
  decreases len(a)
= if len(a) == 0 then 0 else if a[0] == 0 then min_swaps(a[1..len(a)]) else min_swaps(a[1..len(a)]) + 1
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant r == min_swaps(arr[0..i])
    decreases n - i
  {
    if arr[i] == 1 {
      r := r + 1;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}
