task check_alphanumeric(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures r == "Accept" or r == "Discard"
  ensures r == "Accept" ==> (len(s) > 0 and (forall i in [0, len(s)) . (s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9' or s[i] >= 'a' and s[i] <= 'z' or s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z')))
  ensures r == "Discard" ==> (len(s) == 0 or (exists i in [0, len(s)) . not (s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9' or s[i] >= 'a' and s[i] <= 'z' or s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z')))
{
  if len(s) == 0 {
    r := "Discard";
  } else {
    if (forall i in [0, len(s)) . (s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9' or s[i] >= 'a' and s[i] <= 'z' or s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z')) {
      r := "Accept";
    } else {
      r := "Discard";
    }
  }
}

task anagram_lambda(words: seq, target: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) <= len(words)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . i < len(words) ==> r[i] == words[i]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(words)) . i < len(r) ==> r[i] == words[i]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(words)) . (i < len(r) and r[i] == words[i]) ==> (forall j in [0, len(target)) . j < len(words[i]) ==> words[i][j] == target[j]) and len(words[i]) == len(target)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(words)) . (i < len(r) and r[i] == words[i]) ==> (forall j in [0, len(target)) . j < len(words[i]) ==> words[i][j] == target[j]) and len(words[i]) == len(target)
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(words)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(words)
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . k < i ==> r[k] == words[k]
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . k < i ==> (forall j in [0, len(target)) . j < len(words[k]) ==> words[k][j] == target[j]) and len(words[k]) == len(target)
    decreases len(words) - i
  {
    if len(words[i]) == len(target) and (forall j in [0, len(target)) . j < len(words[i]) ==> words[i][j] == target[j]) {
      r := r + [words[i]];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task find_longest_conseq_subseq(s: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires len(s) == n
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures forall i in [0, n) . s[i] >= 0
  ensures r == longest_conseq(s, n)
  decreases n
spec fun longest_conseq(s: seq, n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 0 else if longest_conseq(s, n - 1) >= 1 then longest_conseq(s, n - 1) else 1
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant r >= 0
    invariant r == longest_conseq(s, i)
    decreases n - i
  {
    if i == 0 {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      if r >= 1 {
        r := r;
      } else {
        r := 1;
      }
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task palindrome_lambda(words: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) <= len(words)
  ensures forall k in [0, len(r)) . exists i in [0, len(words)) . r[k] == words[i] and is_pal(words[i])
  ensures forall i in [0, len(words)) . is_pal(words[i]) ==> exists k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == words[i]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(words)) . not is_pal(words[i]) ==> (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] != words[i])
  ensures forall k in [0, len(r)) . exists i in [0, len(words)) . r[k] == words[i] and is_pal(words[i])
spec fun is_pal(s: seq): bool
  decreases len(s)
= forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == s[len(s) - 1 - i]
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(words)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(words)
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, i) . r[k] == words[j] and is_pal(words[j])
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . is_pal(words[j]) ==> exists k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == words[j]
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . not is_pal(words[j]) ==> (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] != words[j])
    decreases len(words) - i
  {
    if is_pal(words[i]) {
      r := r + [words[i]];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant i <= len(arr)

    decreases n - i
  {
    s := s + arr[i];
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if s % 2 == 0 {
    r := 2;
  } else {

  }
}

task fibonacci(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == fibs(n)
  decreases n
spec fun fibs(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then n else fibs(n - 1) + fibs(n - 2)
{
  if n <= 1 {
    r := n;
  } else {
    var a: int := fibonacci(n - 1);
    var b: int := fibonacci(n - 2);
    r := a + b;
  }
}

task check_Concat(s: seq, t: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (len(t) > 0 and len(s) % len(t) == 0 and forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == t[i % len(t)])
{
  if len(t) == 0 {
    r := false;
  } else {
    var ok: bool := true;
    var i: int := 0;
    while i < len(s) and ok
      invariant 0 <= i
      invariant i <= len(s)
      invariant ok ==> (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] == t[j % len(t)])
      decreases len(s) - i
    {
      if s[i] != t[i % len(t)] {
        ok := false;
      } else {
      }
      i := i + 1;
    }
    if ok and len(s) % len(t) == 0 {
      r := true;
    } else {
      r := false;
    }
  }
}

gate recursion
task lcm(a: int, b: int) returns (r: int)
  requires a > 0
  requires b > 0
  ensures r == lcmaux(a, b)
  decreases a + b
spec fun lcmaux(a: int, b: int): int
  decreases a + b
= if a <= 0 then b else if b <= 0 then a else if a > b then lcmaux(a - b, b) else if b > a then lcmaux(a, b - a) else a
{
  var g: int := 0;
  var x: int := a;
  var y: int := b;
  if x == 0 {
    g := y;
  } else {
    if y == 0 {
      g := x;
    } else {
      if x > y {
        g := lcm(x - y, y);
      } else {
        if y > x {
          g := lcm(x, y - x);
        } else {
          g := x;
        }
      }
    }
  }
  r := a * b / g;
}

task sort_String(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] >= 0
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [i + 1, len(r)) . r[i] <= r[j]
  ensures forall c in [0, 1114112) . (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == c) ==> (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == c)
{
  r := s;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(r)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(r)
    invariant len(r) == len(s)
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . forall k in [j + 1, i) . r[j] <= r[k]
    invariant forall c in [0, 1114112) . (forall j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] == c) ==> (forall j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] == c)
    decreases len(r) - i
  {
    var min_idx: int := i;
    var j: int := i + 1;
    while j < len(r)
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= len(r)
      invariant min_idx >= i
      invariant min_idx < len(r)
      invariant r[min_idx] <= r[i]
      invariant forall k in [i, min_idx) . r[k] >= r[min_idx]
      invariant forall k in [min_idx + 1, j) . r[k] >= r[min_idx]
      invariant len(r) == len(s)
      invariant forall c in [0, 1114112) . (forall k in [0, len(s)) . s[k] == c) ==> (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == c)
      decreases len(r) - j
    {
      if r[j] < r[min_idx] {
        min_idx := j;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if min_idx != i {
      var tmp: int := r[i];
      r := r[i := r[min_idx]];
      r := r[min_idx := tmp];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task text_match(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures r == "Found a match!" or r == "Not matched!"
  ensures r == "Found a match!" ==> exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 'a' and exists j in [i, len(s)) . s[j] == 'b'
  ensures r == "Not matched!" ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 'a' ==> (forall j in [i, len(s)) . s[j] != 'b'))
{
  var found: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and not found
    invariant i >= 0
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant not found ==> (forall k in [0, i) . s[k] == 'a' ==> (forall l in [k, i) . l < len(s) ==> s[l] != 'b'))
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == 'a' {
      var j: int := i;
      while j < len(s) and s[j] != 'b'
        invariant j >= i
        invariant j <= len(s)
        invariant (forall k in [i, j) . k < len(s) ==> s[k] != 'b')
        decreases len(s) - j
      {
        j := j + 1;
      }
      if j < len(s) {
        found := true;
      } else {
      }
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if found {
    r := "Found a match!";
  } else {
    r := "Not matched!";
  }
}

    } else {
      r := "No solutions";

  }
}

task sum_even_odd(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  requires len(s) >= 2
  requires exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] % 2 == 1
  requires exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] % 2 == 0
  ensures r == first_odd(s) + first_even(s)
spec fun first_odd(s: seq): int
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) == 0 then 0 else if s[0] % 2 == 1 then s[0] else first_odd(s[1..])
spec fun first_even(s: seq): int
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) == 0 then 0 else if s[0] % 2 == 0 then s[0] else first_even(s[1..])
{
  var first_o: int := 0;
  var first_e: int := 0;
  var found_o: bool := false;
  var found_e: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and (not found_o or not found_e)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant found_o ==> first_o == first_odd(s)
    invariant found_e ==> first_e == first_even(s)
    invariant not found_o ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] % 2 != 1)
    invariant not found_e ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] % 2 != 0)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if not found_o and s[i] % 2 == 1 {
      first_o := s[i];
      found_o := true;
    } else {
    }
    if not found_e and s[i] % 2 == 0 {
      first_e := s[i];
      found_e := true;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := first_o + first_e;
}

task remove_spaces(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) <= len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] != ' '
  ensures len(r) == 0 or r[0] != ' '
  ensures len(r) == 0 or r[len(r) - 1] != ' '
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r) - 1) . r[i] == ' ' ==> r[i + 1] != ' '
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] != ' ' ==> exists j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] == s[i]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] != ' ' ==> exists j in [0, len(s)) . s[j] == r[i]
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  var prev_space: bool := true;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] != ' '
    invariant len(r) == 0 or r[0] != ' '
    invariant len(r) == 0 or r[len(r) - 1] != ' '
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r) - 1) . r[k] == ' ' ==> r[k + 1] != ' '
    invariant prev_space ==> (len(r) == 0 or r[len(r) - 1] == ' ')
    invariant not prev_space ==> (len(r) == 0 or r[len(r) - 1] != ' ')
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . s[j] != ' ' ==> exists k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == s[j]
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] != ' ' ==> exists j in [0, i) . s[j] == r[k]
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == ' ' {
      if not prev_space {
        r := r + [' '];
        prev_space := true;
      } else {
      }
    } else {
      r := r + [s[i]];
      prev_space := false;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if len(r) > 0 and r[0] == ' ' {
    r := r[1..len(r)];
  } else {
  }
  if len(r) > 0 and r[len(r) - 1] == ' ' {
    r := r[0..len(r) - 1];
  } else {
  }
}

task max_sum_subseq(a: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == max_sum_subseq_spec(a)
spec fun max_sum_subseq_spec(a: seq): int
  decreases len(a)
= if len(a) == 0 then 0 else if len(a) == 1 then a[0] else if a[0] > a[1] then max_sum_subseq_spec(a[2..]) + a[0] else max_sum_subseq_spec(a[1..])
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  var prev2: int := 0;
  var prev1: int := 0;
  while i < len(a)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(a)
    invariant prev2 == max_sum_subseq_spec(a[0..i-2])
    invariant prev1 == max_sum_subseq_spec(a[0..i-1])
    decreases len(a) - i
  {
    if i == 0 {
      prev1 := a[0];
    } else {
      if i == 1 {
        if a[0] > a[1] {
          prev1 := a[0];
        } else {
          prev1 := a[1];
        }
      } else {
        if prev2 + a[i] > prev1 {
          prev2 := prev1;
          prev1 := prev2 + a[i];
        } else {
          prev2 := prev1;
          prev1 := prev1;
        }
      }
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := prev1;
}

task check(a: seq, n: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires n == len(a)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == (n == 0 or n == 1 or (a[0] <= a[n-1] ==> (a[0] <= a[n-1] and (a[0] <= a[n-1] ==> true)) or true))
{
  if n <= 1 {
    r := true;
  } else {
    var left: int := 0;
    var right: int := n - 1;
    var last: int := -2147483648;
    var ok: bool := true;
    while left <= right and ok
      invariant 0 <= left
      invariant right < n
      invariant left <= right + 1
      invariant ok ==> (forall i in [0, left) . i < n)
      decreases right - left + 1
    {
      if a[left] <= a[right] {
        if a[left] < last {
          ok := false;
        } else {
          last := a[left];
          left := left + 1;
        }
      } else {
        if a[right] < last {
          ok := false;
        } else {
          last := a[right];
          right := right - 1;
        }
      }
    }
    r := ok;
  }
}

  decreases 0
spec fun max_word_len(words: seq): int
  decreases len(words)
= if len(words) == 0 then 0 else if len(words[0]) > max_word_len(words[1..len(words)]) then len(words[0]) else max_word_len(words[1..len(words)])
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(words)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(words)
    invariant r == max_word_len(words[0..i])
    decreases len(words) - i
  {
    if len(words[i]) > r {
      r := len(words[i]);
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task match_num(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (len(s) >= 1 and s[0] >= '0' and s[0] <= '9' and (len(s) == 1 or s[1] == '-'))
{
  if len(s) >= 1 and s[0] >= '0' and s[0] <= '9' and (len(s) == 1 or s[1] == '-') {
    r := true;
  } else {
    r := false;
  }
}

t 1
gate recursion
task smallest_multiple(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r == lcm1(n)
  decreases n
spec fun lcm1(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 1 then 1 else lcm1(n - 1) * n / gcd(lcm1(n - 1), n)
{
  if n == 1 {
    r := 1;
  } else {
    var prev: int := smallest_multiple(n - 1);
    var g: int := gcd(prev, n);
    r := prev * n / g;
  }
}

t 1
gate loops
task count_Unset_Bits(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r == unset_bits_sum(n)
  decreases n
spec fun unset_bits_sum(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 0 then 0 else unset_bits_sum(n - 1) + unset_bits(n - 1)
spec fun unset_bits(m: int): int
  decreases m
= if m <= 0 then 0 else unset_bits(m - 1) + (1 - bit(m - 1, 0))
spec fun bit(m: int, p: int): int
  decreases m
= if m <= 0 then 0 else bit(m - 1, p - 1)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 1;
  while i <= n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n + 1
    invariant r == unset_bits_sum(i - 1)
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    r := r + unset_bits(i - 1);
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

t 1
gate recursion
task sum_of_square(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == binom_sum_sq(n)
  decreases n
spec fun binom(n: int, k: int): int
  decreases n + k
= if k < 0 or k > n then 0 else if k == 0 or k == n then 1 else binom(n - 1, k - 1) + binom(n - 1, k)
spec fun binom_sum_sq(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n < 0 then 0 else if n == 0 then 1 else binom_sum_sq(n - 1) + binom(n, n - 1) * binom(n, n - 1)
{
  if n == 0 {
    r := 1;
  } else {
    r := sum_of_square(n - 1) + binom(n, n - 1) * binom(n, n - 1);
  }
}

task find_fixed_point(a: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires n <= len(a)
  ensures r == -1 or (r >= 0 and r < n and a[r] == r)
  ensures r == -1 ==> (forall i in [0, n) . a[i] != i)
  ensures r != -1 ==> (forall i in [0, r) . i < n ==> a[i] != i)
{
  r := -1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n and r == -1
    invariant r == -1 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < n ==> a[j] != j)
    invariant r != -1 ==> r >= 0 and r < n and a[r] == r and (forall j in [0, r) . j < n ==> a[j] != j)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= n
    decreases n - i
  {
    if a[i] == i {
      r := i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

t 1
gate recursion
task previous_palindrome(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r < n
  ensures is_pal(r)
  ensures not is_pal(r + 1)
  decreases n
spec fun is_pal(n: int): bool
  decreases n
= if n < 0 then false else if n < 10 then true else if n < 100 then n[0] == n[1] else if n < 1000 then n[0] == n[2] else if n < 10000 then n[0] == n[3] and n[1] == n[2] else if n < 100000 then n[0] == n[4] and n[1] == n[3] else if n < 1000000 then n[0] == n[5] and n[1] == n[4] and n[2] == n[3] else if n < 10000000 then n[0] == n[6] and n[1] == n[5] and n[2] == n[4] else if n < 100000000 then n[0] == n[7] and n[1] == n[6] and n[2] == n[5] and n[3] == n[4] else if n < 1000000000 then n[0] == n[8] and n[1] == n[7] and n[2] == n[6] and n[3] == n[5] else if n < 10000000000 then n[0] == n[9] and n[1] == n[8] and n[2] == n[7] and n[3] == n[6] and n[4] == n[5] else false
{
  if n <= 0 {
    r := -1;
  } else {
    if is_pal(n - 1) {
      r := n - 1;
    } else {
      r := previous_palindrome(n - 1);
    }
  }
}

task check_date(day: int, month: int, year: int) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (month >= 1 and month <= 12 and day >= 1 and day <= 31 and
    (month == 1 or month == 3 or month == 5 or month == 7 or month == 8 or month == 10 or month == 11 ==> day <= 31) and
    (month == 4 or month == 6 or month == 9 or month == 11 ==> day <= 30) and
    (month == 2 ==> (if (year % 4 == 0 and year % 100 != 0) or year % 400 == 0 then day <= 29 else day <= 28)))
{
  var is_leap: bool := (year % 4 == 0 and year % 100 != 0) or year % 400 == 0;
  var max_day: int := 31;
  if month == 4 or month == 6 or month == 9 or month == 11 {
    max_day := 30;
  } else {
    if month == 2 {
      if is_leap {
        max_day := 29;
      } else {
        max_day := 28;
      }
    } else {
    }
  }
  r := month >= 1 and month <= 12 and day >= 1 and day <= max_day;
}

  ensures r == (len(s) > 0 and s[len(s) - 1] >= '0' and s[len(s) - 1] <= '9')

    r := true;
  } else {
    r := false;
  }
}

task is_Two_Alter(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (len(s) >= 2 and s[0] != s[1] and forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == (if i % 2 == 0 then s[0] else s[1]))
{
  if len(s) < 2 {
    r := false;
  } else {
    if s[0] == s[1] {
      r := false;
    } else {
      var ok: bool := true;
      var i: int := 2;
      while i < len(s) and ok
        invariant 0 <= i
        invariant i <= len(s)
        invariant ok ==> (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] == (if j % 2 == 0 then s[0] else s[1]))
        decreases len(s) - i
      {
        if s[i] != (if i % 2 == 0 then s[0] else s[1]) {
          ok := false;
        } else {
        }
        i := i + 1;
      }
      r := ok;
    }
  }
}

task rearrange_numbs(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] >= 0) ==> (forall j in [0, i) . r[j] >= 0)
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] < 0) ==> (forall j in [i, len(r)) . r[j] < 0)
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] >= 0) ==> (forall j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] < 0 ==> i < j)
  ensures (forall x in [0, len(s)) . s[x] >= 0) ==> (forall y in [0, len(r)) . r[y] >= 0 ==> exists z in [0, len(s)) . s[z] == r[y] and s[z] >= 0)
  ensures (forall x in [0, len(s)) . s[x] < 0) ==> (forall y in [0, len(r)) . r[y] < 0 ==> exists z in [0, len(s)) . s[z] == r[y] and s[z] < 0)
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] >= 0) ==> (forall j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] >= 0 ==> i < j ==> r[i] <= r[j])
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] < 0) ==> (forall j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] < 0 ==> i < j ==> r[i] >= r[j])
{
  var pos: seq := [];
  var neg: seq := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant (forall k in [0, i) . s[k] >= 0 ==> exists p in [0, len(pos)) . pos[p] == s[k])
    invariant (forall k in [0, i) . s[k] < 0 ==> exists n in [0, len(neg)) . neg[n] == s[k])
    invariant (forall p in [0, len(pos)) . pos[p] >= 0)
    invariant (forall n in [0, len(neg)) . neg[n] < 0)
    invariant len(pos) + len(neg) == i
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] >= 0 {
      pos := pos + [s[i]];
    } else {
      neg := neg + [s[i]];
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  var pos_sorted: seq := pos;
  var n: int := len(pos_sorted);
  var a: int := 0;
  while a < n
    invariant 0 <= a
    invariant a <= n
    invariant (forall p in [0, a) . pos_sorted[p] >= 0)
    invariant (forall p in [0, a) . (forall q in [0, a) . p < q ==> pos_sorted[p] <= pos_sorted[q]))
    invariant (forall p in [0, a) . exists k in [0, len(pos)) . pos[k] == pos_sorted[p])
    decreases n - a
  {
    var min_idx: int := a;
    var b: int := a + 1;
    while b < n
      invariant a <= min_idx
      invariant min_idx < n
      invariant a <= b
      invariant b <= n
      invariant (forall p in [a, b) . pos_sorted[p] >= pos_sorted[min_idx])
      decreases n - b
    {
      if pos_sorted[b] < pos_sorted[min_idx] {
        min_idx := b;
      } else {
      }
      b := b + 1;
    }
    if min_idx != a {
      var tmp: int := pos_sorted[a];
      pos_sorted := pos_sorted[a := pos_sorted[min_idx]];
      pos_sorted := pos_sorted[min_idx := tmp];
    } else {
    }
    a := a + 1;
  }
  var neg_sorted: seq := neg;
  var m: int := len(neg_sorted);
  var c: int := 0;
  while c < m
    invariant 0 <= c
    invariant c <= m
    invariant (forall p in [0, c) . neg_sorted[p] < 0)
    invariant (forall p in [0, c) . (forall q in [0, c) . p < q ==> neg_sorted[p] >= neg_sorted[q]))
    invariant (forall p in [0, c) . exists k in [0, len(neg)) . neg[k] == neg_sorted[p])
    decreases m - c
  {
    var max_idx: int := c;
    var d: int := c + 1;
    while d < m
      invariant c <= max_idx
      invariant max_idx < m
      invariant c <= d
      invariant d <= m
      invariant (forall p in [c, d) . neg_sorted[p] <= neg_sorted[max_idx])
      decreases m - d
    {
      if neg_sorted[d] > neg_sorted[max_idx] {
        max_idx := d;
      } else {
      }
      d := d + 1;
    }
    if max_idx != c {
      var tmp2: int := neg_sorted[c];
      neg_sorted := neg_sorted[c := neg_sorted[max_idx]];
      neg_sorted := neg_sorted[max_idx := tmp2];
    } else {
    }
    c := c + 1;
  }
  r := pos_sorted + neg_sorted;
}

task text_uppercase_lowercase(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures r == "Found a match!" or r == "Not matched!"
  ensures r == "Found a match!" ==> exists i in [0, len(s) - 1) . s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z' and s[i + 1] >= 'a' and s[i + 1] <= 'z'
  ensures r == "Not matched!" ==> (forall i in [0, len(s) - 1) . not (s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z' and s[i + 1] >= 'a' and s[i + 1] <= 'z'))
{
  var found: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) - 1 and not found
    invariant i >= 0
    invariant i <= len(s) - 1
    invariant not found ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) - 1 ==> not (s[j] >= 'A' and s[j] <= 'Z' and s[j + 1] >= 'a' and s[j + 1] <= 'z'))
    decreases len(s) - 1 - i
  {
    if s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z' and s[i + 1] >= 'a' and s[i + 1] <= 'z' {
      found := true;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if found {
    r := "Found a match!";
  } else {
    r := "Not matched!";
  }
}

task coin_change(coins: seq, target: int, amount: int) returns (r: int)
  requires amount >= 0
  requires forall i in [0, len(coins)) . coins[i] > 0
  ensures r == cc(coins, target, amount)
  decreases amount + (len(coins) - target)
spec fun cc(coins: seq, target: int, amount: int): int
  decreases amount + (len(coins) - target)
= if amount == 0 then 1 else if target >= len(coins) then 0 else if amount < coins[target] then cc(coins, target + 1, amount) else cc(coins, target, amount - coins[target]) + cc(coins, target + 1, amount)
{
  if amount == 0 {
    r := 1;
  } else {
    if target >= len(coins) {
      r := 0;
    } else {
      if amount < coins[target] {
        r := coin_change(coins, target + 1, amount);
      } else {
        var a: int := coin_change(coins, target, amount - coins[target]);
        var b: int := coin_change(coins, target + 1, amount);
        r := a + b;
      }
    }
  }
}

task multiply_list(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == prod(s)
spec fun prod(s: seq): int
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) == 0 then 1 else s[0] * prod(s[1..len(s)])
{
  r := 1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant r == prod(s[0..i])
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    r := r * s[i];
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task is_undulating(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  requires len(s) >= 1
  ensures r == (len(s) >= 2 and (forall i in [0, len(s) - 2) . s[i] != s[i + 1] and s[i + 1] != s[i + 2]))
{
  if len(s) < 2 {
    r := false;
  } else {
    r := true;
    var i: int := 0;
    while i < len(s) - 2 and r
      invariant 0 <= i
      invariant i <= len(s) - 2
      invariant r ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) - 2 ==> s[j] != s[j + 1] and s[j + 1] != s[j + 2])
      decreases len(s) - 2 - i
    {
      if s[i] == s[i + 1] or s[i + 1] == s[i + 2] {
        r := false;
      } else {
      }
      i := i + 1;
    }
  }
}

task super_seq(s1: seq, s2: seq, n1: int, n2: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n1 == len(s1)
  requires n2 == len(s2)
  requires n1 >= 0
  requires n2 >= 0
  ensures r == ss_len(s1, s2, n1, n2)
  decreases n1 + n2
spec fun ss_len(s1: seq, s2: seq, n1: int, n2: int): int
  decreases n1 + n2
= if n1 == 0 then n2 else if n2 == 0 then n1 else if s1[n1 - 1] == s2[n2 - 1] then 1 + ss_len(s1, s2, n1 - 1, n2 - 1) else 1 + (if ss_len(s1, s2, n1 - 1, n2) <= ss_len(s1, s2, n1, n2 - 1) then ss_len(s1, s2, n1 - 1, n2) else ss_len(s1, s2, n1, n2 - 1))
{
  if n1 == 0 {
    r := n2;
  } else {
    if n2 == 0 {
      r := n1;
    } else {
      if s1[n1 - 1] == s2[n2 - 1] {
        r := 1 + super_seq(s1, s2, n1 - 1, n2 - 1);
      } else {
        var a: int := super_seq(s1, s2, n1 - 1, n2);
        var b: int := super_seq(s1, s2, n1, n2 - 1);
        if a <= b {
          r := 1 + a;
        } else {
          r := 1 + b;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

  ensures r == x or r == y

    r := x;
  } else {
    r := y;
  }
}

gate recursion
task rencontres_number(n: int, k: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires k >= 0
  requires k <= n
  ensures r == rn(n, k)
  decreases n + k
spec fun rn(n: int, k: int): int
  decreases n + k
= if n == 0 then if k == 0 then 1 else 0 else if k == 0 then rn(n - 1, 1) + rn(n - 1, 0) else rn(n - 1, k - 1) + (n - 1) * rn(n - 1, k)
{
  if n == 0 {
    if k == 0 {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      r := 0;
    }
  } else {
    if k == 0 {
      var a: int := 0;
      var b: int := 0;
      a := rencontres_number(n - 1, 1);
      b := rencontres_number(n - 1, 0);
      r := a + b;
    } else {
      var c: int := 0;
      var d: int := 0;
      c := rencontres_number(n - 1, k - 1);
      d := rencontres_number(n - 1, k);
      r := c + (n - 1) * d;
    }
  }
}

  decreases b
= if b == 0 then 1 else a * pow(a, b - 1)

    r := 1;
  } else {
    r := a * power(a, b - 1);

}

task text_match(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures r == "Found a match!" or r == "Not matched!"
  ensures r == "Found a match!" ==> exists i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 'a' and (forall j in [i + 1, len(s)) . s[j] != 'b')
  ensures r == "Not matched!" ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] != 'a' or (exists j in [i + 1, len(s)) . s[j] == 'b')
{
  var found: bool := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and not found
    invariant (forall k in [0, i) . s[k] != 'a' or (exists j in [k + 1, len(s)) . s[j] == 'b'))
    invariant found ==> exists k in [0, i) . s[k] == 'a' and (forall j in [k + 1, len(s)) . s[j] != 'b')
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == 'a' {
      var j: int := i + 1;
      var all_b: bool := true;
      while j < len(s) and all_b
        invariant (forall k in [i + 1, j) . s[k] == 'b')
        invariant all_b ==> (forall k in [i + 1, j) . s[k] == 'b')
        decreases len(s) - j
      {
        if s[j] != 'b' {
          all_b := false;
        } else {
        }
        j := j + 1;
      }
      if all_b {
        found := true;
      } else {
      }
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if found {
    r := "Found a match!";
  } else {
    r := "Not matched!";
  }
}

task sum_series(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == (n * (n + 1) / 2) * (n * (n + 1) / 2)
{

  var i: int := 1;
  while i <= n
    invariant i >= 1
    invariant i <= n + 1
    invariant r == (i - 1) * i * (2 * i - 1) / 6
    decreases n - i + 1
  {
    r := r + i * i * i;
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task remove_duplic_list(words: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) <= len(words)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, len(words)) . words[j] == r[i]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [0, i) . r[i] != r[j]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(words)) . exists j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] == words[i]
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(words)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(words)
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, i) . words[j] == r[k]
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . forall m in [0, k) . r[k] != r[m]
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . exists j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] == words[k]
    decreases len(words) - i
  {
    var found: bool := false;
    var j: int := 0;
    while j < len(r) and not found
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= len(r)
      invariant not found ==> (forall m in [0, j) . r[m] != words[i])
      invariant found ==> exists m in [0, j) . r[m] == words[i]
      decreases len(r) - j
    {
      if r[j] == words[i] {
        found := true;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if found {
    } else {
      r := r + [words[i]];
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task camel_to_snake(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s) + (len(s) - 1)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] >= 'a' and r[i] <= 'z' or r[i] == '_'
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) == i + (i - 1)
    invariant forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] >= 'a' and r[k] <= 'z' or r[k] == '_'
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if i > 0 and s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z' {
      r := r + ['_'];
    } else {
    }
    if s[i] >= 'A' and s[i] <= 'Z' {
      r := r + [s[i] + 32];
    } else {
      r := r + [s[i]];
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task dealnnoy_num(m: int, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires m >= 0
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == delannoy(m, n)
  decreases m + n
spec fun delannoy(m: int, n: int): int
  decreases m + n
= if m == 0 and n == 0 then 1 else if m == 0 then delannoy(0, n - 1) else if n == 0 then delannoy(m - 1, 0) else delannoy(m - 1, n) + delannoy(m, n - 1) + delannoy(m - 1, n - 1)
{
  if m == 0 and n == 0 {
    r := 1;
  } else {
    if m == 0 {
      r := dealnnoy_num(0, n - 1);
    } else {
      if n == 0 {
        r := dealnnoy_num(m - 1, 0);
      } else {
        var a: int := dealnnoy_num(m - 1, n);
        var b: int := dealnnoy_num(m, n - 1);
        var c: int := dealnnoy_num(m - 1, n - 1);
        r := a + b + c;
      }
    }
  }
}

task heap_sort(a: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(a)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . forall j in [0, len(r)) . i < j ==> r[i] <= r[j]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(a)) . exists j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] == a[i]
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . exists j in [0, len(a)) . a[j] == r[i]
{
  var n: int := len(a);
  var arr: seq := a;
  var i: int := n / 2 - 1;
  while i >= 0
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n / 2
    invariant len(arr) == n
    decreases n / 2 - i
  {
    var j: int := i;
    var largest: int := i;
    var left: int := 2 * i + 1;
    var right: int := 2 * i + 2;
    if left < n and arr[left] > arr[largest] {
      largest := left;
    } else {
    }
    if right < n and arr[right] > arr[largest] {
      largest := right;
    } else {
    }
    if largest != i {
      var tmp: int := arr[i];
      arr := arr[i := arr[largest]];
      arr := arr[largest := tmp];
    } else {
    }
    i := i - 1;
  }
  var end: int := n - 1;
  while end > 0
    invariant 0 <= end
    invariant end <= n
    invariant len(arr) == n
    invariant forall i in [0, end) . forall j in [0, end) . i < j ==> arr[i] <= arr[j]
    invariant forall i in [0, end) . forall j in [end, n) . arr[i] <= arr[j]
    decreases end
  {
    var tmp: int := arr[0];
    arr := arr[0 := arr[end]];
    arr := arr[end := tmp];
    var k: int := 0;
    var largest: int := 0;
    var left: int := 1;
    var right: int := 2;
    while 2 * k + 1 < end
      invariant 0 <= k
      invariant k < end
      invariant 2 * k + 1 < end
      invariant forall i in [0, k) . forall j in [0, k) . i < j ==> arr[i] <= arr[j]
      invariant forall i in [0, k) . forall j in [k, end) . arr[i] <= arr[j]
      invariant forall i in [end, n) . forall j in [0, end) . arr[j] <= arr[i]
      decreases end - k
    {
      largest := k;
      if arr[left] > arr[largest] {
        largest := left;
      } else {
      }
      if right < end and arr[right] > arr[largest] {
        largest := right;
      } else {
      }
      if largest != k {
        var tmp2: int := arr[k];
        arr := arr[k := arr[largest]];
        arr := arr[largest := tmp2];
        k := largest;
        left := 2 * k + 1;
        right := 2 * k + 2;
      } else {
      }
    }
    end := end - 1;
  }
  r := arr;
}

gate loops
task combine_lists(a: seq, b: seq) returns (r: seq)
  requires forall i in [0, len(a)) . i == 0 or a[i-1] <= a[i]
  requires forall i in [0, len(b)) . i == 0 or b[i-1] <= b[i]
  ensures len(r) == len(a) + len(b)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . i == 0 or r[i-1] <= r[i]
  ensures forall x in [0, len(a)) . (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == a[x]) == (exists j in [0, len(a)) . a[j] == a[x] and (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == a[x] ==> (forall m in [0, len(a)) . a[m] == a[x] ==> m == x or m == j)))
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == a[0]) ==> len(a) > 0
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == b[0]) ==> len(b) > 0
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == a[0]) ==> len(a) > 0 and (forall j in [0, len(a)) . a[j] == a[0] ==> (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == a[0] ==> (forall m in [0, len(a)) . a[m] == a[0] ==> m == j)))
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == b[0]) ==> len(b) > 0 and (forall j in [0, len(b)) . b[j] == b[0] ==> (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == b[0] ==> (forall m in [0, len(b)) . b[m] == b[0] ==> m == j)))
  ensures (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == a[0]) ==> len(a) > 0 and (forall j in [0, len(a)) . a[j] == a[0] ==> (forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == a[0] ==> (forall m in [0, len(a)) . a[m] == a[0] ==> m == j)))

task num_position(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == -1 or r >= 0 and r < len(s) and s[r] >= '0' and s[r] <= '9'
  ensures r == -1 ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . not (s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9'))
  ensures r != -1 ==> (forall i in [0, r) . not (s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9'))
{
  r := -1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and r == -1
    invariant r == -1 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . not (s[j] >= '0' and s[j] <= '9'))
    invariant r != -1 ==> r >= 0 and r < len(s) and s[r] >= '0' and s[r] <= '9' and (forall j in [0, r) . not (s[j] >= '0' and s[j] <= '9'))
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] >= '0' and s[i] <= '9' {
      r := i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task len_log(words: seq) returns (r: int)
  requires len(words) > 0
  ensures r == (forall i in [0, len(words)) . len(words[i]) >= r) and (exists i in [0, len(words)) . len(words[i]) == r)
{
  r := len(words[0]);
  var i: int := 1;
  while i < len(words)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(words)
    invariant (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(words) ==> len(words[j]) >= r)
    invariant (exists j in [0, i) . j < len(words) and len(words[j]) == r)
    decreases len(words) - i
  {
    if len(words[i]) < r {
      r := len(words[i]);
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task nCr_mod_p(n: int, r: int, p: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires 0 <= r <= n
  requires p > 0
  ensures 0 <= r < p
  ensures r == nCr_val(n, r) mod p
spec fun nCr_val(n: int, r: int): int
  decreases n + r
= if r == 0 then 1 else if r == n then 1 else nCr_val(n-1, r-1) + nCr_val(n-1, r)
{
  var c: int := 1;
  var k: int := 1;
  while k <= r
    invariant 0 <= k
    invariant k <= r
    invariant c == nCr_val(n, k-1)
    decreases r - k + 1
  {
    c := c * (n - k + 1);
    var q: int := 0;
    var rem: int := c;
    while rem >= k
      invariant rem >= 0
      invariant c == q * k + rem
      decreases rem
    {
      q := q + 1;
      rem := rem - k;
    }
    c := q;
    k := k + 1;
  }
  var res: int := c;
  while res >= p
    invariant res >= 0
    invariant c == res + (c - res)
    invariant 0 <= c - res
    decreases res
  {
    res := res - p;
  }
  r := res;
}

task subset(s: seq, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires len(s) == n
  ensures r >= 1
  ensures r == max_freq(s)
spec fun max_freq(s: seq): int
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) == 0 then 1 else if max_freq(s[0..len(s)-1]) > count_in(s[0..len(s)-1], s[len(s)-1]) then max_freq(s[0..len(s)-1]) else count_in(s[0..len(s)-1], s[len(s)-1])
spec fun count_in(s: seq, x: int): int
  decreases len(s)
= if len(s) == 0 then 0 else if s[len(s)-1] == x then 1 + count_in(s[0..len(s)-1], x) else count_in(s[0..len(s)-1], x)
{
  r := 1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant r >= 1
    invariant r == max_freq(s[0..i])
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    var cnt: int := 0;
    var j: int := 0;
    while j < i
      invariant 0 <= j
      invariant j <= i
      invariant cnt == count_in(s[0..j], s[i])
      decreases i - j
    {
      if s[j] == s[i] {
        cnt := cnt + 1;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    if cnt + 1 > r {
      r := cnt + 1;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task is_abundant(n: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires n >= 1
  ensures r == (sum_proper_divisors(n) > n)
spec fun sum_proper_divisors(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then 0 else 1 + (if n == 2 then 0 else sum_proper_divisors(n - 1))
{
  r := false;
  var s: int := 0;
  var i: int := 1;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= n
    invariant s == sum_proper_divisors(i)
    decreases n - i
  {
    if n - i > 0 and (n - i) * i == n {
      s := s + i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if s > n {
    r := true;
  } else {
    r := false;
  }
}

task get_First_Set_Bit_Pos(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n > 0
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures (1 << r) <= n
  ensures (1 << (r + 1)) > n
{
  r := 0;
  var x: int := n;
  while x > 1
    invariant x > 0
    invariant (1 << r) <= n
    invariant (1 << (r + 1)) > n or x > 1
    decreases x
  {
    x := x >> 1;
    r := r + 1;
  }
}

task get_noOfways(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == ways(n)
  decreases n
spec fun ways(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n == 0 then 1 else if n == 1 then 1 else if n == 2 then 2 else if n == 3 then 2 else ways(n - 2) + ways(n - 3)
{
  if n == 0 {
    r := 1;
  } else {
    if n == 1 {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      if n == 2 {
        r := 2;
      } else {
        if n == 3 {
          r := 2;
        } else {
          var a: int := get_noOfways(n - 2);
          var b: int := get_noOfways(n - 3);
          r := a + b;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

task sum_Even(l: int, r: int) returns (r: int)
  requires l <= r
  ensures r == sum_even_spec(l, r)
spec fun sum_even_spec(l: int, r: int): int
  decreases r - l
= if l > r then 0 else if l % 2 == 0 then l + sum_even_spec(l + 2, r) else sum_even_spec(l + 1, r)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := l;
  while i <= r
    invariant i >= l
    invariant i <= r + 1
    invariant r == sum_even_spec(l, i - 1)
    decreases r - i + 1
  {
    if i % 2 == 0 {
      r := r + i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task word_len(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (len(s) % 2 == 0)
{
  var i: int := 0;
  var even: bool := true;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant i >= 0
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant even == (i % 2 == 0)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    even := not even;
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := even;
}

task check(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures r == "accepted" or r == "not accepted"
  ensures r == "accepted" ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 'a' or s[i] == 'e' or s[i] == 'i' or s[i] == 'o' or s[i] == 'u' or s[i] == 'A' or s[i] == 'E' or s[i] == 'I' or s[i] == 'O' or s[i] == 'U')
  ensures r == "not accepted" ==> (exists i in [0, len(s)) . not (s[i] == 'a' or s[i] == 'e' or s[i] == 'i' or s[i] == 'o' or s[i] == 'u' or s[i] == 'A' or s[i] == 'E' or s[i] == 'I' or s[i] == 'O' or s[i] == 'U'))
{
  var all_vowels: bool := true;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and all_vowels
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant all_vowels ==> (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] == 'a' or s[j] == 'e' or s[j] == 'i' or s[j] == 'o' or s[j] == 'u' or s[j] == 'A' or s[j] == 'E' or s[j] == 'I' or s[j] == 'O' or s[j] == 'U')
    invariant not all_vowels ==> (exists j in [0, i) . not (s[j] == 'a' or s[j] == 'e' or s[j] == 'i' or s[j] == 'o' or s[j] == 'u' or s[j] == 'A' or s[j] == 'E' or s[j] == 'I' or s[j] == 'O' or s[j] == 'U'))
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if not (s[i] == 'a' or s[i] == 'e' or s[i] == 'i' or s[i] == 'o' or s[i] == 'u' or s[i] == 'A' or s[i] == 'E' or s[i] == 'I' or s[i] == 'O' or s[i] == 'U') {
      all_vowels := false;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  if all_vowels {
    r := "accepted";
  } else {
    r := "not accepted";
  }
}

t 0
task min_of_two(x: int, y: int) returns (r: int)
  ensures r <= x
  ensures r <= y
  ensures r == x or r == y
{
  if x <= y {
    r := x;
  } else {
    r := y;
  }
}

gate loops
task maximum_segments(n: int, a: int, b: int, c: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires a > 0
  requires b > 0
  requires c > 0
  ensures r == -1 or r >= 0
  ensures r == -1 ==> (forall x in [0, n/a + 1) . forall y in [0, (n - x*a)/b + 1) . x*a + y*b + (n - x*a - y*b) % c != 0 or (n - x*a - y*b) < 0)
  ensures r != -1 ==> (exists x in [0, n/a + 1) . exists y in [0, (n - x*a)/b + 1) . x*a + y*b + (n - x*a - y*b) % c == 0 and (n - x*a - y*b) >= 0 and x + y + (n - x*a - y*b) / c == r)
{
  r := -1;
  var x: int := 0;
  while x * a <= n
    invariant x >= 0
    invariant x * a <= n
    invariant r == -1 ==> (forall i in [0, x) . forall j in [0, (n - i*a)/b + 1) . i*a + j*b > n or (n - i*a - j*b) % c != 0)
    invariant r != -1 ==> (exists i in [0, x) . exists j in [0, (n - i*a)/b + 1) . i*a + j*b <= n and (n - i*a - j*b) % c == 0 and i + j + (n - i*a - j*b) / c == r)
    decreases n - x * a
  {
    var y: int := 0;
    while y * b <= n - x * a
      invariant y >= 0
      invariant y * b <= n - x * a
      invariant r == -1 ==> (forall i in [0, x) . forall j in [0, (n - i*a)/b + 1) . i*a + j*b > n or (n - i*a - j*b) % c != 0) and (forall j in [0, y) . x*a + j*b > n - x*a or (n - x*a - j*b) % c != 0)
      invariant r != -1 ==> (exists i in [0, x) . exists j in [0, (n - i*a)/b + 1) . i*a + j*b <= n and (n - i*a - j*b) % c == 0 and i + j + (n - i*a - j*b) / c == r) or (exists j in [0, y) . x*a + j*b <= n - x*a and (n - x*a - j*b) % c == 0 and x + j + (n - x*a - j*b) / c == r)
      decreases (n - x * a) - y * b
    {
      var rem: int := n - x * a - y * b;
      if rem % c == 0 {
        var z: int := rem / c;
        var count: int := x + y + z;
        if count > r {
          r := count;
        } else {
        }
      } else {
      }
      y := y + 1;
    }
    x := x + 1;
  }
}

gate quantifiers
task left_rotate(s: seq, k: int) returns (r: seq)
  requires k >= 0
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . r[i] == s[(i + k) % len(s)]
{
  var n: int := len(s);
  if n == 0 {
    r := [];
  } else {
    var kk: int := k;
    while kk >= n
      invariant kk >= 0
      decreases kk - n
    {
      kk := kk - n;
    }
    r := s[kk..n] + s[0..kk];
  }
}

gate loops
task decimal_to_binary(n: int) returns (r: seq)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures n == 0 ==> r == "0"
  ensures n > 0 ==> len(r) > 0
  ensures n > 0 ==> (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == '0' or r[i] == '1')
  ensures n > 0 ==> r[0] == '1'
  ensures n == (sum_bits(r, 0))
spec fun sum_bits(s: seq, i: int): int
  decreases len(s) - i
= if i >= len(s) then 0 else (if s[i] == '1' then 1 else 0) * (2 ^ (len(s) - 1 - i)) + sum_bits(s, i + 1)
{
  if n == 0 {
    r := "0";
  } else {
    r := [];
    var m: int := n;
    while m > 0
      invariant m >= 0
      invariant n == (sum_bits(r, 0)) + m * (2 ^ len(r))
      invariant (forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] == '0' or r[i] == '1')
      decreases m
    {
      if m % 2 == 1 {
        r := [ '1' ] + r;
        m := (m - 1) / 2;
      } else {
        r := [ '0' ] + r;
        m := m / 2;
      }
    }
  }
}
